activity_nars_orgio = {
	is_shown = {
		highest_held_title_tier > tier_barony
		is_landed = yes
		is_ai = no
		age >= 18
	}

	can_start_showing_failures_only = {
		is_available_adult = yes
	}

	is_valid = {
		NOT = {
			has_variable = activity_invalidated
		}
		scope:host = {
			is_landed = yes
			NOT = { is_incapable = yes }
			is_ai = no
			age >= 18
		}
		# If no one shows up
		trigger_if = {
			limit = {
				is_current_phase_active = yes
			}
			has_attending_activity_guests = yes
		}
	}

	on_invalidated = {
		# Host becomes unlanded
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:host = { is_landed = no }
			}
			scope:activity = {
				activity_type = { save_scope_as = activity_type }
			}
			every_attending_character = {
				trigger_event = activity_system.0320
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:host = { is_incapable = yes }
			}
			scope:activity = {
				activity_type = { save_scope_as = activity_type }
			}
			scope:host = {
				trigger_event = activity_system.0330
			}
			every_attending_character = {
				limit = { NOT = { this = scope:host } }
				trigger_event = activity_system.0331
			}
		}
		if = { # No one shows up
			limit = {
				has_attending_activity_guests = no
			}
			scope:activity = {
				activity_type = { save_scope_as = activity_type }
				activity_location = { save_scope_as = location }
			}
			scope:host = { trigger_event = activity_system.0100 }
		}
	}

	on_host_death = {
		scope:activity = {
			set_variable = {
				name = activity_invalidated
			}
		}
	}

	ai_province_filter = capital
	wait_time_before_start = { months = 1 }

	###################
	# OPTIONS
	###################

	options = {
		orgio_option_luxuries = {
			orgio_luxuries_normal = {
				is_shown = { has_dlc_feature = advanced_activities }
				# Paid 4/5 in gold and 1/5 in piety.
				cost = {
					gold = {
						add = {
							value = nars_orgio_luxuries_normal_cost
							multiply = 0.8
							desc = orgio_luxuries_normal
						}
					}
					piety = {
						add = {
							value = nars_orgio_luxuries_normal_cost
							multiply = 0.2
							desc = orgio_luxuries_normal
						}
					}
				}

				ai_will_do = {
					value = 0
					# Financial preference.
					add = activity_option_likes_cheap_expense_value
					# Fanciness preference.
					add = activity_option_likes_functional_food_option_value
				}
			}
			orgio_luxuries_enhanced = {
				default = yes
				# Paid 4/5 in gold and 1/5 in piety.
				cost = {
					gold = {
						add = {
							value = nars_orgio_luxuries_enhanced_cost
							multiply = 0.8
							desc = orgio_luxuries_enhanced
						}
					}
					piety = {
						add = {
							value = nars_orgio_luxuries_enhanced_cost
							multiply = 0.2
							desc = orgio_luxuries_enhanced
						}
					}
				}

				ai_will_do = {
					value = 0
					# Financial preference.
					add = activity_option_likes_middling_expense_value
					# Fanciness preference.
					## We just use slightly reduced fanciness prefs here.
					add = {
						value = activity_option_likes_ostentatious_food_option_value
						multiply = 0.5
					}
				}
			}
			orgio_luxuries_lavish = {
				is_shown = { has_dlc_feature = advanced_activities }
				# Paid 4/5 in gold and 1/5 in piety.
				cost = {
					gold = {
						add = {
							value = nars_orgio_luxuries_lavish_cost
							multiply = 0.8
							desc = orgio_luxuries_lavish
						}
					}
					piety = {
						add = {
							value = nars_orgio_luxuries_lavish_cost
							multiply = 0.2
							desc = orgio_luxuries_lavish
						}
					}
				}

				ai_will_do = {
					value = 0
					# Financial preference.
					add = activity_option_likes_heavy_expense_value
					# Fanciness preference.
					add = activity_option_likes_ostentatious_food_option_value
				}
			}
		}
	}

	###################
	# PHASES
	###################

	phases = {
		# Blessing: the incense is burned before the revels begin.
		nars_orgio_blessing_phase = {
			order = 1
			is_predefined = yes

			on_phase_active = {
				if = {
					limit = { this = scope:host }
					scope:activity = { progress_activity_phase_after = { days = 5 } }
				}
				# Too few guests: tell the host, which invalidates the activity.
				if = {
					limit = {
						this = scope:host
						scope:activity = {
							any_attending_character = {
								NOT = { this = scope:host }
								count < 4
							}
						}
					}
					trigger_event = nars_orgio.3002
				}
				# Otherwise every participant breathes the incense.
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						scope:activity = {
							any_attending_character = {
								NOT = { this = scope:host }
								count >= 4
							}
						}
					}
					trigger_event = {
						id = nars_orgio.3005
						days = 1
					}
				}
			}
		}

		# Orgio: the revels themselves.
		nars_orgio_phase = {
			order = 2
			is_predefined = yes

			on_enter_phase = {
				if = {
					limit = { this = scope:host }
					# Same location as the blessing, so skip the passive state.
					scope:activity = { progress_activity_phase_after = { days = 1 } }
				}
			}
			
			on_phase_active = {
				if = {
					limit = { this = scope:host }
					scope:activity = { progress_activity_phase_after = { weeks = 2 } }
					# Check that there is a female guest apart from the host.
					if = {
						limit = {
							scope:activity = {
								any_attending_character = {
									NOT = {	this = scope:host }
									count < 4
								}
							}
						}
						# If there are no enough guests, throw this event.
						trigger_event = nars_orgio.3002
					}
					# If there are guests present
					else = {
						play_music_cue = "mx_cue_sacredrite"
						
						#chance for the whore being brought
						random = {
							chance = 20
							trigger_event = {
								id = nars_orgio.3003
								days = 1
							}
						}

						# (also sends event to every participant based on outcome)
						##After, participants can choose what they want to do during the orgy
						trigger_event = {
							id = nars_orgio.3010
							days = 7
						}

						# The host may overhear a guest's secret; Luxuries set the odds.
						nars_orgio_host_secret_roll_effect = yes
					}
				}
			}

			on_end = {
				# Run conclusion stuff here
				if = {
					limit = { this = scope:host }
					trigger_event = nars_orgio.3098
				}
				else = { trigger_event =  nars_orgio.3099 }
			}
		}
	}

	max_guest_arrival_delay_time = { months = 2 } 


	province_filter = capital

	max_province_icons = 5
	
	cost = {
		gold = {
			add = {
				value = activity_minor_gold_value
				desc = hunt_base_cost
			}
		}
	}

	ui_predicted_cost = {
		gold = {
			# Base Cost
			value = activity_minor_gold_value
			# Make it a multiple of 5 (rounded up)
			divide = 5 
			ceiling = yes 
			multiply = 5
		}
	}
	cooldown = { days = 100 }
	
	###################
	# ACTIVITY Special stuff
	###################
	
	on_start = {
		set_variable = {
			name = host_var
			value = scope:host
		}
		add_activity_log_entry = {
			key = excited_to_start
			character = scope:host
			scope:host = {
				stress_impact = {
					base = minor_stress_gain
					lustful = minor_stress_loss
					sadistic = minor_stress_loss
					shy = medium_stress_gain
					chaste = medium_stress_gain
					lazy = minor_stress_gain
				}
			}
		}
	}

	# GUEST HANDLING
	###################

	max_guests = 20

	guest_invite_rules = {
		defaults = {
			2 = activity_invite_rule_courtiers
			2 = activity_invite_rule_guests
			2 = activity_invite_rule_vassals
			2 = activity_invite_rule_lovers

			6 = activity_invite_rule_liege
			6 = activity_invite_rule_fellow_vassals
			9 = activity_invite_rule_extended_family
			9 = activity_invite_spouses
		}
		rules = {
			# MP 
			6 = activity_invite_mp
		}
	}

	can_be_activity_guest = {
		is_adult = yes
		is_available = yes
		in_diplomatic_range = scope:host
		age >= 18
	}

	host_intents = {
		intents = { revel_incarnalpleasure_intent }
		default = revel_incarnalpleasure_intent
	}

	guest_intents = {
		intents = { revel_incarnalpleasure_intent }
		default = revel_incarnalpleasure_intent
	}

	guest_join_chance = {
		base = -50
		base_activity_modifier = yes

		modifier = {
			has_trait = lustful
			add = 100
    	}
		modifier = {
			has_trait = sadistic
			add = 50
    	}
		modifier = {
			has_trait = chaste
			add = -50
    	}
		modifier = {
			has_trait = shy
			add = -50
    	}
		modifier = {
			has_trait = craven
			add = -20
    	}
		modifier = {
			has_trait = deviant
			add = 100
    	}
		modifier = {
			rite = {
				rite_has_doctrine = doctrine_adultery_women_accepted
			}
			add = 20
    	}
		modifier = {
			rite = {
				rite_has_doctrine = doctrine_adultery_men_accepted
			}
			add = 20
    	}

		# Scripted Modifiers
		activity_guest_shared_ai_accept_modifier = yes
	}
	
	on_complete = {
		if = {
			limit = { this = scope:host }
			scope:host = {
				disburse_nars_orgio_host_rewards_effect = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			disburse_nars_orgio_guest_rewards_effect = yes
		}
	}

						scope:host.location = scope:activity.activity_location

				}
			}

				triggered_animation = {

							NOT = { has_trait = craven }
						}
					}
					animation = { boredom shame flirtation lunatic admiration toast_goblet drink scheme spymaster ecstasy }
				}
				animation = shame
			}
		}

					animation = { idle }
				}
				triggered_animation = {
					trigger = { scope:character = { has_trait = impatient } }
					animation = { jockey_gallop }
				}

			}
		}

			}

					trigger = {

						}
					}
					animation = { boredom flirtation lunatic admiration toast_goblet drink scheme spymaster ecstasy }

				animation = shame
			}
		}

﻿selian_incursion_cb = {
    icon = struggle
    group = invasion

    target_title_tier = all

    interface_priority = 360

    allow_hostages = no

    allowed_for_character = {
        has_trait = nars_hegemon
        is_selian = yes
        is_archaic = yes
    }

    allowed_for_character_display_regardless = {
        prestige_level >= 3
    }

    allowed_against_character_display_regardless = {
        scope:defender = {
            is_independent_ruler = yes
            is_landed = yes
        }
    }

    allowed_against_character = {
        scope:attacker = {
            ALL_FALSE = {
                top_liege = scope:defender.top_liege
                liege = scope:defender
            }
        }
        scope:defender = {
            NOR = {
                government_has_flag = government_is_tribal
                government_has_flag = government_is_nomadic
                government_has_flag = government_is_mandala
                government_has_flag = government_is_wanua
                is_selian = yes
                is_archaic = yes
                is_ethnically_selian = yes
            }
        }
    }

    should_invalidate = {
        scope:defender = {
            OR = {
                is_independent_ruler = no
                is_landed = no
            }
        }
    }

    on_invalidated_desc = msg_subjugation_war_invalidated_message

    on_invalidated = {
    }

    cost = {
    }

    on_declaration = {
        on_declared_war = yes
    }

    on_victory_desc = {
        desc = {
            first_valid = {
                triggered_desc = {
                    trigger = { scope:attacker = { is_local_player = yes } }
                    desc = selian_incursion_victory_attacker
                }
                triggered_desc = {
                    trigger = { scope:defender = { is_local_player = yes } }
                    desc = selian_incursion_victory_defender
                }
                desc = selian_incursion_victory
            }
        }
    }

    on_victory = {
        scope:attacker = { save_scope_as = gok }
        scope:defender = {
            save_scope_as = gok_victim
            primary_title = { save_scope_as = target_primary_title }
            capital_county = {
                save_scope_as = target_capital_county
                duchy = { save_scope_as = target_capital_duchy }
            }

            if = {
                limit = {
                    OR = {
                        any_held_title = { has_variable = refused_submission }
                        any_vassal_or_below = { any_held_title = { has_variable = refused_submission } }
                    }
                }
                save_scope_as = khans_retribution_target
            }
        }

        ## --- 1. COMPILE HOLDINGS TO TAKE (Defender's entire sub-realm) ---
        hidden_effect = {
            # Save old family vassals for later use in follower pool
            scope:attacker = {
                every_vassal_or_below = {
                    limit = { dynasty = scope:attacker.dynasty }
                    add_to_list = vassalised_family_list
                }
            }

            scope:defender = {
                every_held_title = {
                    limit = { tier >= tier_duchy }
                    add_to_list = titles_to_take
                }
                every_vassal_or_below = {
                    every_held_title = {
                        limit = { tier >= tier_duchy }
                        add_to_list = titles_to_take
                    }
                }
                every_sub_realm_county = { add_to_list = counties_to_take }
            }
        }

        # WRAPPED IN HIDDEN_EFFECT TO PREVENT UI SPAM
        hidden_effect = {
            ## --- 2. THE GREAT SWEEP (Usurp everything into attacker's hands) ---
            create_title_and_vassal_change = {
                type = conquest
                save_scope_as = wipe_change
                add_claim_on_loss = yes
            }

            # Take all upper titles (Empires, Kingdoms, Duchies)
            every_in_list = {
                list = titles_to_take
                change_title_holder = { holder = scope:attacker change = scope:wipe_change }
            }

            # Take all counties
            every_in_list = {
                list = counties_to_take
                change_title_holder = {
                    holder = scope:attacker
                    change = scope:wipe_change
                    take_baronies = yes
                }
            }

            resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:wipe_change

            ## --- 3. ENSURE PRIMARY TITLE & CAPITAL DUCHY EXIST ---
            if = {
                limit = {
                    exists = scope:target_primary_title
                    scope:target_primary_title = { is_title_created = no }
                }
                create_title_and_vassal_change = {
                    type = created
                    save_scope_as = create_prim_change
                    add_claim_on_loss = no
                }
                scope:target_primary_title = {
                    change_title_holder = { holder = scope:attacker change = scope:create_prim_change }
                }
                resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:create_prim_change
            }
            if = {
                limit = {
                    exists = scope:target_capital_duchy
                    scope:target_capital_duchy = { is_title_created = no }
                }
                create_title_and_vassal_change = {
                    type = created
                    save_scope_as = create_duchy_change
                    add_claim_on_loss = no
                }
                scope:target_capital_duchy = {
                    change_title_holder = { holder = scope:attacker change = scope:create_duchy_change }
                }
                resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:create_duchy_change
            }
        }

        ## --- 5. DISTRIBUTION SETUP ---
        hidden_effect = {
            # Build duchy distribution list: every duchy from taken counties,
            # excluding the capital duchy which the attacker keeps
            every_in_list = {
                list = counties_to_take
                duchy = {
                    if = {
                        limit = {
                            NOT = { is_in_list = duchies_to_distribute }
                            NOT = { this = scope:target_capital_duchy }
                        }
                        add_to_list = duchies_to_distribute
                    }
                }
            }

            # Build follower pool: attacker's adult courtiers + pre-existing family vassals
            scope:attacker = {
                every_courtier = {
                    limit = { is_adult = yes is_imprisoned = no }
                    add_to_list = follower_pool
                }
            }
            every_in_list = { list = vassalised_family_list add_to_list = follower_pool }
        }

        ## --- 6. DISTRIBUTE DUCHIES & COUNTIES to followers ---
        hidden_effect = {
            every_in_list = {
                list = duchies_to_distribute
                save_temporary_scope_as = duchy_to_give

                # Generate the Duchy if it doesn't currently exist
                if = {
                    limit = { is_title_created = no }
                    create_title_and_vassal_change = {
                        type = created
                        save_scope_as = create_d_change
                        add_claim_on_loss = no
                    }
                    change_title_holder = { holder = scope:attacker change = scope:create_d_change }
                    resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:create_d_change
                }

                # Setup fail-safe blank scope
                hidden_effect = {
                    random_in_list = {
                        list = follower_pool
                        limit = { always = no }
                        save_temporary_scope_as = chosen_follower
                    }
                }

                # Priority 1: Family members of the attacker's dynasty
                if = {
                    limit = {
                        any_in_list = {
                            list = follower_pool
                            NOT = { is_in_list = used_followers }
                            dynasty = scope:attacker.dynasty
                        }
                    }
                    hidden_effect = {
                        random_in_list = {
                            list = follower_pool
                            limit = {
                                NOT = { is_in_list = used_followers }
                                dynasty = scope:attacker.dynasty
                            }
                            save_temporary_scope_as = chosen_follower
                        }
                    }
                }
                # Priority 2: Characters with inheritable positive traits
                else_if = {
                    limit = {
                        any_in_list = {
                            list = follower_pool
                            NOT = { is_in_list = used_followers }
                            OR = {
                                has_trait = beauty_good_1
                                has_trait = beauty_good_2
                                has_trait = beauty_good_3
                                has_trait = intellect_good_1
                                has_trait = intellect_good_2
                                has_trait = intellect_good_3
                                has_trait = physique_good_1
                                has_trait = physique_good_2
                                has_trait = physique_good_3
                                has_trait = fecund
                                has_trait = pure_blooded
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    hidden_effect = {
                        random_in_list = {
                            list = follower_pool
                            limit = {
                                NOT = { is_in_list = used_followers }
                                OR = {
                                    has_trait = beauty_good_1
                                    has_trait = beauty_good_2
                                    has_trait = beauty_good_3
                                    has_trait = intellect_good_1
                                    has_trait = intellect_good_2
                                    has_trait = intellect_good_3
                                    has_trait = physique_good_1
                                    has_trait = physique_good_2
                                    has_trait = physique_good_3
                                    has_trait = fecund
                                    has_trait = pure_blooded
                                }
                            }
                            save_temporary_scope_as = chosen_follower
                        }
                    }
                }
                # Priority 3: Characters with good stats (12+)
                else_if = {
                    limit = {
                        any_in_list = {
                            list = follower_pool
                            NOT = { is_in_list = used_followers }
                            OR = {
                                martial >= 12
                                stewardship >= 12
                                diplomacy >= 12
                                intrigue >= 12
                                learning >= 12
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    hidden_effect = {
                        random_in_list = {
                            list = follower_pool
                            limit = {
                                NOT = { is_in_list = used_followers }
                                OR = {
                                    martial >= 12
                                    stewardship >= 12
                                    diplomacy >= 12
                                    intrigue >= 12
                                    learning >= 12
                                }
                            }
                            save_temporary_scope_as = chosen_follower
                        }
                    }
                }
                # Priority 4: Any remaining unused follower
                else_if = {
                    limit = {
                        any_in_list = {
                            list = follower_pool
                            NOT = { is_in_list = used_followers }
                        }
                    }
                    hidden_effect = {
                        random_in_list = {
                            list = follower_pool
                            limit = { NOT = { is_in_list = used_followers } }
                            save_temporary_scope_as = chosen_follower
                        }
                    }
                }
                # Priority 5: Generate a new Selian character from template
                else = {
                    hidden_effect = {
                        create_character = {
                            location = scope:attacker.capital_province
                            template = selian_template.1001
                            save_temporary_scope_as = chosen_follower
                        }
                        scope:chosen_follower = {
                            set_employer = scope:attacker
                            nars_apply_setup_effect = yes
                        }
                    }
                }

                # Execute grants — duchy title + all de jure counties within it
                if = {
                    limit = { exists = scope:chosen_follower }
                    hidden_effect = {
                        scope:chosen_follower = { add_to_list = used_followers }
                    }

                    create_title_and_vassal_change = {
                        type = granted
                        save_scope_as = duchy_grant_change
                        add_claim_on_loss = no
                    }

                    scope:duchy_to_give = {
                        change_title_holder = {
                            holder = scope:chosen_follower
                            change = scope:duchy_grant_change
                        }
                    }

                    # Grant all de jure counties in this duchy that the attacker holds
                    scope:duchy_to_give = {
                        every_in_de_jure_hierarchy = {
                            limit = {
                                tier = tier_county
                                holder = scope:attacker
                                is_in_list = counties_to_take
                            }
                            change_title_holder = {
                                holder = scope:chosen_follower
                                change = scope:duchy_grant_change
                                take_baronies = yes
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:duchy_grant_change
                }
            }
        }

        ## --- 7. FINAL INCURSION MECHANICS ---
        scope:attacker = {
            custom_tooltip = selian_incursion_gain_titles_tt

            scope:defender = {
                pay_short_term_gold = {
                    target = scope:attacker
                    gold = major_gold_value
                }
                gok_realm_devastation_effect = yes
            }

            gok_retribution_cleanup_effect = yes
            show_pow_release_message_effect = yes
        }
        add_legitimacy_attacker_victory_effect = yes

        if = {
            limit = { scope:defender = { sub_realm_size >= 20 } }
            scope:attacker = { accolade_attacker_war_end_glory_gain_high_effect = yes }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { scope:defender = { sub_realm_size >= 10 } }
            scope:attacker = { accolade_attacker_war_end_glory_gain_med_effect = yes }
        }
        else = {
            scope:attacker = { accolade_attacker_war_end_glory_gain_low_effect = yes }
        }

        modify_allies_of_participants_fame_values = {
            WINNER = scope:attacker
            FAME_BASE = mongol_invasion_cb_ally_prestige
            IS_RELIGIOUS_WAR = no
            WINNER_ALLY_FAME_SCALE = 1
            LOSER_ALLY_FAME_SCALE = 3
        }

        scope:attacker = {
            add_prestige_experience = {
                value = mongol_invasion_cb_prestige_experience_gain
            }
        }

        scope:defender = {
            add_prestige = {
                value = major_prestige_value
                multiply = -1.0
            }
        }

        scope:attacker = { fp1_remember_recent_conquest_victory_effect = yes }
        laamp_as_mercenary_payout_tooltip_effect = yes
    }

    on_white_peace_desc = {
        desc = {
            first_valid = {
                triggered_desc = {
                    trigger = { scope:defender = { is_local_player = yes } }
                    desc = selian_incursion_white_peace_defender
                }
                desc = selian_incursion_white_peace
            }
        }
    }

    on_white_peace = {
        scope:defender = {
            every_sub_realm_county = {
                limit = { county_controller = scope:attacker }
                add_to_list = occupied_counties
            }
            create_title_and_vassal_change = {
                type = conquest
                save_scope_as = change
                add_claim_on_loss = yes
            }
            every_in_list = {
                list = occupied_counties
                change_title_holder = {
                    holder = scope:attacker
                    change = scope:change
                }
            }
            resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:change
        }

        scope:attacker = { show_pow_release_message_effect = yes }
        scope:defender = { accolade_defender_war_end_glory_gain_med_effect = yes }

        modify_allies_of_participants_fame_values = {
            WINNER = scope:attacker
            FAME_BASE = mongol_invasion_cb_ally_prestige
            IS_RELIGIOUS_WAR = no
            WINNER_ALLY_FAME_SCALE = 1
            LOSER_ALLY_FAME_SCALE = 5
        }

        add_truce_white_peace_effect = yes

        scope:attacker = {
            add_prestige = major_prestige_loss
            add_legitimacy = minor_legitimacy_loss
            stress_impact = {
                ambitious = minor_stress_impact_gain
                arrogant = minor_stress_impact_gain
            }
        }

        laamp_as_mercenary_payout_tooltip_effect = yes
        gok_retribution_cleanup_effect = yes
    }

    on_defeat_desc = {
        desc = {
            first_valid = {
                triggered_desc = {
                    trigger = { scope:attacker = { is_local_player = yes } }
                    desc = selian_incursion_defeat_attacker
                }
                triggered_desc = {
                    trigger = { scope:defender = { is_local_player = yes } }
                    desc = selian_incursion_defeat_defender
                }
                desc = selian_incursion_defeat
            }
        }
    }

    on_defeat = {
        scope:attacker = { show_pow_release_message_effect = yes }

        scope:defender = {
            mandala_peacemaker_perk_serenity_effect = yes
            accolade_defender_war_end_glory_gain_high_effect = yes
        }

        modify_allies_of_participants_fame_values = {
            WINNER = scope:defender
            FAME_BASE = mongol_invasion_cb_ally_prestige
            IS_RELIGIOUS_WAR = no
            WINNER_ALLY_FAME_SCALE = 1
            LOSER_ALLY_FAME_SCALE = 10
        }

        add_truce_attacker_defeat_effect = yes

        scope:defender = {
            if = {
                limit = {
                    OR = {
                        is_ai = no
                        scope:attacker = { is_ai = no }
                    }
                }

                add_legitimacy_attacker_defeat_effect = yes

                scope:attacker = {
                    add_prestige = massive_prestige_loss
                    add_legitimacy = major_legitimacy_loss
                    stress_impact = {
                        ambitious = medium_stress_impact_gain
                        arrogant = medium_stress_impact_gain
                    }
                    pay_short_term_gold_reparations_effect = { GOLD_VALUE = 5 }
                    every_vassal = {
                        custom = custom.every_vassal
                        add_opinion = {
                            modifier = disapproval
                            target = prev
                            opinion = -40
                            years = 4
                        }
                    }
                    save_temporary_scope_as = loser
                }

                add_prestige = mongol_invasion_cb_prestige_gain
                on_lost_aggression_war_discontent_loss = yes
            }
        }
        selian_minor_county_effect = yes
        laamp_as_mercenary_payout_tooltip_effect = yes
        gok_retribution_cleanup_effect = yes
    }

    transfer_behavior = transfer

    on_primary_attacker_death = inherit
    on_primary_defender_death = inherit

    attacker_allies_inherit = yes
    defender_allies_inherit = yes

    war_name = "SELIAN_INCURSION_WAR_NAME"
    my_war_name = "SELIAN_INCURSION_WAR_NAME_MY"
    war_name_base = "SELIAN_INCURSION_WAR_NAME_BASE"
    cb_name = "SELIAN_INCURSION_CB_NAME"

    ticking_war_score_targets_entire_realm = yes
    attacker_wargoal_percentage = 0.8
    attacker_ticking_warscore = 0
    max_defender_score_from_occupation = 150
    max_attacker_score_from_occupation = 150
    attacker_score_from_occupation_scale = 50
    defender_score_from_occupation_scale = 50

    max_ai_diplo_distance_to_title = 500
}

selian_migration_cb = {
    icon = struggle
    group = invasion

    interface_priority = 180

    combine_into_one = yes
    should_show_war_goal_subview = yes
    mutually_exclusive_titles = { always = yes }

    allowed_for_character = {
        is_selian = yes
        government_has_flag = government_is_landless_adventurer
        custom_tooltip = {
            text = selian_migration_global_limit_tt
            OR = {
                NOT = { has_global_variable = selian_migration_global_count }
                global_var:selian_migration_global_count < 3
            }
        }
    }

    allowed_for_character_display_regardless = {
        is_at_war = no
        tgp_japan_offensive_wars_ban_trigger = yes
    }

    allowed_against_character = {
        scope:attacker = {
            ALL_FALSE = {
                top_liege = scope:defender.top_liege
                liege = scope:defender
            }
        }
        scope:defender = {
            NOT = {  }
            NOR = {
                is_selian = yes
                is_archaic = yes
                is_ethnically_selian = yes
            }
        }
    }
    
    target_titles = all
    target_title_tier = kingdom
    target_de_jure_regions_above = yes # Allows the engine to grab unformed kingdoms inside an Empire
    ignore_effect = change_title_holder

    # RESTRICTS TARGETS TO ONLY 100% CONTROLLED KINGDOMS (FORMED OR UNFORMED)
    valid_to_target_title = {
        scope:defender = {
            completely_controls = prev
        }
    }

    ai_can_target_all_titles = { always = yes }
    
    ai_score_mult = {
        value = varangian_adventure_ai_targeting_value
        add = frankokratia_leader_protection_value
        add = {
            if = {
                limit = {
                    scope:defender = {
                        OR = {
                            government_has_flag = government_is_tribal
                            government_has_flag = government_is_nomadic
                        }
                    }
                }
                add = -1000
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = {
                current_military_strength > 2200
            }
            add = 1000
        }
    }
    
    valid_to_start = {
    }

    should_invalidate = {
        NOT = {
            any_in_list = {
                list = target_titles
                any_in_de_jure_hierarchy = {
                    tier = tier_county
                    holder = {
                        target_is_same_character_or_above = scope:defender
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    on_invalidated_desc = msg_county_conquest_war_invalidated_message
    
    on_invalidated = {}

    cost = {
        piety = {
            value = fp1_varangian_adventure_base_piety_cost
            multiply = common_cb_piety_cost_multiplier
        }
        prestige = {
            value = varangian_adventurer_cb_cost
            multiply = common_cb_prestige_cost_multiplier
        }
    }

    on_declaration = {
        on_declared_war = yes
        scope:attacker = {
            capital_barony = { save_scope_as = capital_barony }
        }

        # GLOBAL LIMIT FIX: Increment global declaration tally
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { has_global_variable = selian_migration_global_count } }
            set_global_variable = { name = selian_migration_global_count value = 1 }
        }
        else = {
            change_global_variable = { name = selian_migration_global_count add = 1 }
        }
    }

    on_victory_desc = {
        desc = {            
            first_valid = {
                triggered_desc = {
                    trigger = { scope:attacker = { is_local_player = yes } }
                    desc = selian_migration_victory_attacker
                }
                triggered_desc = {
                    trigger = { scope:defender = { is_local_player = yes } }
                    desc = selian_migration_victory_defender
                }
                desc = selian_migration_victory
            }
        }
    }

    on_victory = {
        custom_tooltip = selian_migration_tt
        # Legitimacy & Glory
        add_legitimacy_attacker_victory_effect = yes
        scope:attacker = { accolade_attacker_war_end_glory_gain_med_effect = yes }

        scope:attacker = {
            if = { limit = { exists = liege this != liege } liege = { save_scope_as = title_beneficiary } }
            
            # Save old family vassals for later cleanup (Hidden to reduce spam)
            hidden_effect = {
                every_vassal_or_below = { limit = { dynasty = scope:attacker.dynasty } add_to_list = vassalised_family_list }
            }

            ## --- 1. IDENTIFY TARGET KINGDOM & CAPITAL DUCHY ---
            random_in_list = {
                list = target_titles
                limit = { tier = tier_kingdom }
                save_scope_as = target_kingdom
            }
            if = {
                limit = { exists = scope:target_kingdom }
                scope:target_kingdom.title_capital_county = { save_scope_as = target_capital_county }
                scope:target_capital_county.duchy = { save_scope_as = target_capital_duchy }
            }

            ## --- 2. PRE-COMPILE HOLDINGS INTO LISTS ---
            hidden_effect = {
                every_in_list = {
                    list = target_titles
                    every_in_de_jure_hierarchy = {
                        continue = { tier > tier_county }
                        limit = {
                            exists = holder
                            holder != scope:attacker
                            holder = { OR = { this = scope:defender target_is_liege_or_above = scope:defender } }
                        }
                        if = {
                            limit = { tier = tier_county }
                            add_to_list = counties_to_take
                        }
                        else_if = {
                            limit = { tier = tier_duchy }
                            add_to_list = duchies_to_take
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            # WRAPPED IN HIDDEN_EFFECT TO PREVENT UI SPAM
            hidden_effect = {
                ## --- 3. THE GREAT SWEEP (Attacker strips realm completely bare) ---
                create_title_and_vassal_change = { type = conquest save_scope_as = wipe_change add_claim_on_loss = yes }
                
                # Take all targeted counties and duchies into the Attacker's hands
                every_in_list = { list = counties_to_take change_title_holder = { holder = scope:attacker change = scope:wipe_change take_baronies = yes } }
                every_in_list = { list = duchies_to_take change_title_holder = { holder = scope:attacker change = scope:wipe_change } }

                # Take the Kingdom if it exists
                if = {
                    limit = { exists = scope:target_kingdom scope:target_kingdom = { is_title_created = yes } }
                    scope:target_kingdom = { change_title_holder = { holder = scope:attacker change = scope:wipe_change } }
                }

                resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:wipe_change

                ## --- 4. ENSURE KINGDOM AND CAPITAL DUCHY EXIST ---
                if = {
                    limit = { exists = scope:target_kingdom scope:target_kingdom = { is_title_created = no } }
                    create_title_and_vassal_change = { type = created save_scope_as = create_king_change add_claim_on_loss = no }
                    scope:target_kingdom = { change_title_holder = { holder = scope:attacker change = scope:create_king_change } }
                    resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:create_king_change
                }
                if = {
                    limit = { exists = scope:target_capital_duchy scope:target_capital_duchy = { is_title_created = no } }
                    create_title_and_vassal_change = { type = created save_scope_as = create_duchy_change add_claim_on_loss = no }
                    scope:target_capital_duchy = { change_title_holder = { holder = scope:attacker change = scope:create_duchy_change } }
                    resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:create_duchy_change
                }
                
                # Fallback titular kingdom
                if = {
                    limit = { NOT = { exists = scope:target_kingdom } }
                    create_dynamic_title = { tier = kingdom name = ADVENTURER_KINGDOM_NAME }
                    create_title_and_vassal_change = { type = created save_scope_as = king_change add_claim_on_loss = no }
                    scope:new_title = {
                        set_destroy_on_gain_same_tier = yes set_no_automatic_claims = yes set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
                        change_title_holder = { holder = scope:attacker change = scope:king_change }
                        save_scope_as = target_kingdom
                    }
                    resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:king_change
                    scope:new_title = { generate_coa = yes }
                }
            }

            ## --- 5. SET NEW CAPITAL ---
            if = {
                limit = { exists = scope:target_capital_county }
                scope:attacker = { set_realm_capital = scope:target_capital_county }
            }

            ## --- 6. DISTRIBUTE FOLLOWER DUCHIES ---
            hidden_effect = {
                every_courtier = { limit = { is_adult = yes is_imprisoned = no } add_to_list = follower_pool }
                every_in_list = { list = vassalised_family_list add_to_list = follower_pool }
            }

            if = {
                limit = { exists = scope:target_kingdom }
                scope:target_kingdom = {
                    every_in_de_jure_hierarchy = {
                        limit = { 
                            tier = tier_duchy
                            NOT = { this = scope:target_capital_duchy }
                        }
                        save_temporary_scope_as = duchy_to_give
                        
                        # Create the Duchy if it doesn't currently exist
                        if = {
                            limit = { is_title_created = no }
                            create_title_and_vassal_change = { type = created save_scope_as = create_d_change add_claim_on_loss = no }
                            change_title_holder = { holder = scope:attacker change = scope:create_d_change }
                            resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:create_d_change
                        }

                        # Setup fail-safe blank scope
                        hidden_effect = {
                            random_in_list = { list = follower_pool limit = { always = no } save_temporary_scope_as = chosen_follower }
                        }

                        # Priority 1: Family
                        if = {
                            limit = { any_in_list = { list = follower_pool NOT = { is_in_list = used_followers } dynasty = scope:attacker.dynasty } }
                            hidden_effect = { random_in_list = { list = follower_pool limit = { NOT = { is_in_list = used_followers } dynasty = scope:attacker.dynasty } save_temporary_scope_as = chosen_follower } }
                        }
                        # Priority 2: Inheritable Traits
                        else_if = {
                            limit = {
                                any_in_list = {
                                    list = follower_pool NOT = { is_in_list = used_followers }
                                    OR = {
                                        has_trait = beauty_good_1 has_trait = beauty_good_2 has_trait = beauty_good_3
                                        has_trait = intellect_good_1 has_trait = intellect_good_2 has_trait = intellect_good_3
                                        has_trait = physique_good_1 has_trait = physique_good_2 has_trait = physique_good_3
                                        has_trait = fecund has_trait = pure_blooded
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                            hidden_effect = {
                                random_in_list = {
                                    list = follower_pool
                                    limit = {
                                        NOT = { is_in_list = used_followers }
                                        OR = {
                                            has_trait = beauty_good_1 has_trait = beauty_good_2 has_trait = beauty_good_3
                                            has_trait = intellect_good_1 has_trait = intellect_good_2 has_trait = intellect_good_3
                                            has_trait = physique_good_1 has_trait = physique_good_2 has_trait = physique_good_3
                                            has_trait = fecund has_trait = pure_blooded
                                        }
                                    }
                                    save_temporary_scope_as = chosen_follower
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        # Priority 3: Good Stats
                        else_if = {
                            limit = { any_in_list = { list = follower_pool NOT = { is_in_list = used_followers } OR = { martial >= 12 stewardship >= 12 diplomacy >= 12 intrigue >= 12 learning >= 12 } } }
                            hidden_effect = { random_in_list = { list = follower_pool limit = { NOT = { is_in_list = used_followers } OR = { martial >= 12 stewardship >= 12 diplomacy >= 12 intrigue >= 12 learning >= 12 } } save_temporary_scope_as = chosen_follower } }
                        }
                        # Priority 4: Any Follower
                        else_if = {
                            limit = { any_in_list = { list = follower_pool NOT = { is_in_list = used_followers } } }
                            hidden_effect = { random_in_list = { list = follower_pool limit = { NOT = { is_in_list = used_followers } } save_temporary_scope_as = chosen_follower } }
                        }
                        # Priority 5: Generate New Character (Using Selian Template)
                        else = {
                            hidden_effect = {
                                create_character = { location = scope:attacker.capital_province template = selian_template.1001 save_temporary_scope_as = chosen_follower }
                                scope:chosen_follower = { set_employer = scope:attacker nars_apply_setup_effect = yes }
                            }
                        }

                        # Execute Duchy and County Grant
                        if = {
                            limit = { exists = scope:chosen_follower }
                            hidden_effect = {
                                scope:chosen_follower = { add_to_list = used_followers }
                                if = {
                                    limit = { scope:chosen_follower = { is_in_list = vassalised_family_list } }
                                    scope:chosen_follower = { add_to_list = vassal_family_processed_list }
                                }
                            }

                            create_title_and_vassal_change = { type = granted save_scope_as = duchy_grant_change add_claim_on_loss = no }
                            
                            # Grant the Duchy title
                            scope:duchy_to_give = { 
                                change_title_holder = { holder = scope:chosen_follower change = scope:duchy_grant_change } 
                            }
                            
                            # Grant all De Jure Counties within this Duchy that the Attacker now holds
                            scope:duchy_to_give = {
                                every_in_de_jure_hierarchy = {
                                    limit = { 
                                        tier = tier_county 
                                        holder = scope:attacker 
                                        is_in_list = counties_to_take
                                    }
                                    change_title_holder = { holder = scope:chosen_follower change = scope:duchy_grant_change take_baronies = yes }
                                }
                            }
                            
                            resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:duchy_grant_change
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            ## --- 7. OLD COUNTRY CLEANUP (HIDDEN TO REDUCE SPAM) ---
            hidden_effect = {
                if = {
                    limit = { exists = scope:target_kingdom }
                    if = {
                        limit = {
                            any_held_title = {
                                title_tier = duchy is_head_of_faith = no NOT = { scope:target_kingdom = { is_de_jure_liege_or_above_target = prev } }
                            }
                        }
                        every_held_title = {
                            title_tier = duchy
                            limit = { is_head_of_faith = no NOT = { scope:target_kingdom = { is_de_jure_liege_or_above_target = prev } } }
                            save_temporary_scope_as = excess_duchy
                            scope:attacker = {
                                every_vassal = {
                                    limit = { NOT = { scope:target_kingdom = { is_de_jure_liege_or_above_target = prev.primary_title } } }
                                    add_to_temporary_list = vassal_transfer
                                }
                                if = {
                                    limit = { exists = scope:title_beneficiary }
                                    scope:excess_duchy = {
                                        create_title_and_vassal_change = { type = granted save_scope_as = change add_claim_on_loss = no }
                                        change_title_holder_include_vassals = { holder = scope:title_beneficiary change = scope:change }
                                        every_in_list = { limit = { this != scope:title_beneficiary } list = vassal_transfer change_liege = { liege = scope:title_beneficiary change = scope:change } }
                                        resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:change
                                    }
                                }
                                else = { destroy_title = scope:excess_duchy }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    if = {
                        limit = { any_held_title = { title_tier = kingdom NOR = { this = scope:target_kingdom is_head_of_faith = yes } } }
                        every_held_title = {
                            title_tier = kingdom
                            limit = { NOR = { this = scope:target_kingdom is_head_of_faith = yes } }
                            save_temporary_scope_as = excess_kingdom
                            scope:attacker = {
                                every_vassal = { limit = { NOT = { scope:target_kingdom = { is_de_jure_liege_or_above_target = prev.primary_title } } } add_to_temporary_list = vassal_transfer }
                                if = {
                                    limit = { exists = scope:title_beneficiary }
                                    scope:excess_kingdom = {
                                        create_title_and_vassal_change = { type = granted save_scope_as = change add_claim_on_loss = no }
                                        change_title_holder_include_vassals = { holder = scope:title_beneficiary change = scope:change }
                                        every_in_list = { limit = { this != scope:title_beneficiary } list = vassal_transfer change_liege = { liege = scope:title_beneficiary change = scope:change } }
                                        resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:change
                                    }
                                }
                                else = { destroy_title = scope:excess_kingdom }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    if = {
                        limit = { any_held_title = { title_tier = empire is_head_of_faith = no } }
                        every_held_title = {
                            title_tier = empire
                            limit = { is_head_of_faith = no }
                            save_temporary_scope_as = excess_empire
                            scope:attacker = {
                                every_vassal = { limit = { NOT = { scope:target_kingdom = { is_de_jure_liege_or_above_target = prev.primary_title } } } add_to_temporary_list = vassal_transfer }
                                if = {
                                    limit = { exists = scope:title_beneficiary }
                                    scope:excess_empire = {
                                        create_title_and_vassal_change = { type = granted save_scope_as = change add_claim_on_loss = no }
                                        change_title_holder_include_vassals = { holder = scope:title_beneficiary change = scope:change }
                                        every_in_list = { limit = { this != scope:title_beneficiary } list = vassal_transfer change_liege = { liege = scope:title_beneficiary change = scope:change } }
                                        resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:change
                                    }
                                }
                                else = { destroy_title = scope:excess_empire }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                if = {
                    limit = { top_liege != this }
                    create_title_and_vassal_change = { type = granted save_scope_as = change add_claim_on_loss = no }
                    becomes_independent = { change = scope:change }
                    resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:change
                }
            }

            # Distribute attacker's old domains to peasant vassals (HIDDEN)
            hidden_effect = {
                every_held_title = {
                    title_tier = county
                    limit = { OR = { NOT = { exists = scope:target_kingdom } kingdom = { this != scope:target_kingdom } } }
                    save_temporary_scope_as = current_county
                    if = {
                        limit = { exists = scope:title_beneficiary }
                        scope:title_beneficiary = { save_temporary_scope_as = local_warlord }
                    }
                    else = { create_character = { location = scope:current_county.title_province template = selian_template.1001 save_temporary_scope_as = local_warlord } }
                    create_title_and_vassal_change = { type = granted save_scope_as = change add_claim_on_loss = no }
                    scope:current_county = { change_title_holder = { holder = scope:local_warlord change = scope:change take_baronies = yes } }
                    resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:change
                    if = {
                        limit = { scope:local_warlord = { is_vassal_of = scope:attacker } }
                        create_title_and_vassal_change = { type = independency save_scope_as = change add_claim_on_loss = no }
                        scope:local_warlord = { becomes_independent = { change = scope:change } save_scope_as = truce_loc add_truce_both_ways = { character = scope:attacker days = 14600 name = CE1_ADVENTURER_TRUCE } nars_apply_setup_effect = yes }
                        resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:change
                    }
                }

                # Distribute landed family's domains outside of the colony to peasant vassals
                every_in_list = {
                    list = vassal_family_processed_list
                    every_held_title = {
                        title_tier = county
                        limit = { OR = { NOT = { exists = scope:target_kingdom } kingdom = { this != scope:target_kingdom } } }
                        save_temporary_scope_as = current_county
                        create_character = { location = scope:current_county.title_province template = selian_template.1001 save_temporary_scope_as = local_warlord }
                        create_title_and_vassal_change = { type = usurped save_scope_as = change add_claim_on_loss = no }
                        scope:current_county = { change_title_holder = { holder = scope:local_warlord change = scope:change take_baronies = yes } }
                        resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:change
                        create_title_and_vassal_change = { type = independency save_scope_as = change add_claim_on_loss = no }
                        scope:local_warlord = { becomes_independent = { change = scope:change } save_scope_as = truce_loc add_truce_both_ways = { character = scope:attacker days = 14600 name = CE1_ADVENTURER_TRUCE } nars_apply_setup_effect = yes }
                        resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:change
                    }
                }

                # Give all old vassals outside of the target_titles independence
                every_vassal = {
                    limit = { OR = { NOT = { exists = scope:target_kingdom } NOT = { any_held_title = { kingdom = scope:target_kingdom } } } }
                    create_title_and_vassal_change = { type = independency save_scope_as = change add_claim_on_loss = no }
                    becomes_independent = { change = scope:change } save_scope_as = truce_loc add_truce_both_ways = { character = scope:attacker days = 14600 name = CE1_ADVENTURER_TRUCE }
                    resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:change
                }
            }

            ## --- 8. FINAL MECHANICS & CLEANUP ---
            scope:attacker = {
                custom_tooltip = fp1_varangian_adventurer_conquest.summaries.control_rebate
                trigger_event = fp1_varangian_adventures.0001
                add_gold = varangian_adventures_bounty_amount_value
                selian_settle_county_effect = yes
                
                hidden_effect = {
                    every_in_list = { list = vassalised_family_list limit = { NOT = { is_in_list = vassal_family_processed_list } } add_to_list = vassal_family_not_processed_list }
                    every_courtier = { limit = { is_ai = yes } add_to_list = vassal_family_not_processed_list }
                    
                    every_in_list = {
                        list = vassal_family_not_processed_list limit = { is_ruler = no } save_temporary_scope_as = landless_family
                        scope:attacker = { add_courtier = scope:landless_family } set_location = scope:attacker.capital_province
                    }
                }
                
                if = {
                    limit = { NOR = { has_trait = adventurer government_has_flag = government_is_nomadic } }
                    add_trait = adventurer
                }
                
                if = {
                    limit = { NOT = { has_variable = va_cb_tally } }
                    set_variable = { name = va_cb_tally value = 2 }
                }
                else = { change_variable = { name = va_cb_tally add = 1 } }
                
                if = {
                    limit = { has_trait = dwarf NOT = { capital_county = { title_province = { geographical_region = world_europe } } } }
                    add_achievement_flag_effect = { FLAG = a_dangerous_business_achievement_flag }
                }
            }

            # Break alliances now out of diplo range
            if = {
                limit = { scope:attacker = { any_ally = { NOT = { in_diplomatic_range = scope:attacker } } } }
                scope:attacker = { every_ally = { limit = { NOT = { in_diplomatic_range = scope:attacker } } break_alliance = scope:attacker } }
            }

            add_truce_attacker_victory_effect = yes
            scope:attacker = { fp1_remember_recent_conquest_victory_effect = yes }
            laamp_as_mercenary_payout_tooltip_effect = yes
            mandala_war_victory_effects = yes
        }
    }

    on_defeat_desc = {
        desc = {            
            first_valid = {
                triggered_desc = {
                    trigger = { scope:attacker = { is_local_player = yes } }
                    desc = selian_migration_defeat_attacker
                }
                triggered_desc = {
                    trigger = { scope:defender = { is_local_player = yes } }
                    desc = selian_migration_defeat_defender
                }
                desc = selian_migration_defeat
            }
        }
    }

    on_defeat = {
        add_legitimacy_attacker_defeat_effect = yes
        
        # DEFENDER REWARDS
        scope:defender = { 
            mandala_peacemaker_perk_serenity_effect = yes 
            accolade_defender_war_end_glory_gain_high_effect = yes 
            add_prestige_war_defender_effect = { PRESTIGE_VALUE = medium_prestige_value }
            add_gold = monumental_gold_value # Hefty sum for defender
        }
        
        # ATTACKER HUGE PENALTIES
        scope:attacker = { 
            remove_short_term_gold = monumental_gold_value # Massive Gold Penalty
            add_prestige = { value = monumental_prestige_value multiply = -1.0 } # Massive Prestige Penalty
        }
        
        modify_allies_of_participants_fame_values = { WINNER = scope:defender FAME_BASE = medium_prestige_value IS_RELIGIOUS_WAR = no WINNER_ALLY_FAME_SCALE = 1 LOSER_ALLY_FAME_SCALE = 1 }
        add_truce_attacker_defeat_effect = yes
        scope:attacker = { save_temporary_scope_as = loser }
        on_lost_aggression_war_discontent_loss = yes
        laamp_as_mercenary_payout_tooltip_effect = yes
        mandala_war_defeat_effects = yes
    }

    transfer_behavior = transfer
    on_primary_attacker_death = inherit
    on_primary_defender_death = inherit
    attacker_allies_inherit = yes
    defender_allies_inherit = yes

    war_name = "SELIAN_MIGRATION_WAR_NAME"
    my_war_name = "SELIAN_MIGRATION_WAR_NAME_MY"
    war_name_base = "SELIAN_MIGRATION_WAR_NAME_BASE"
    cb_name = "SELIAN_MIGRATION_CB_NAME"

    use_de_jure_wargoal_only = yes
    attacker_wargoal_percentage = 0.8
    max_defender_score_from_occupation = 150
    max_attacker_score_from_occupation = 150
}

selian_defiance_cb = {
    icon = struggle
    group = event

    full_occupation_by_defender_gives_victory = no
    attacker_capital_gives_war_score = no
    check_all_defenders_for_ticking_war_score = yes
    defender_ticking_warscore_delay = { months = 4 }
    defender_ticking_warscore = 0.125
    imprisonment_by_defender_give_war_score = no
    defender_score_from_occupation_scale = 50
    attacker_score_from_occupation_scale = 50
    defender_score_from_battles_scale = 125
    attacker_score_from_battles_scale = 125
    ticking_war_score_targets_entire_realm = yes

    occupation_participation_mult = 0.5
    siege_participation_mult = 1
    battle_participation_mult = 7.5

    allow_hostages = no

    allowed_against_character = {
        is_landed = yes
        highest_held_title_tier >= tier_kingdom
    }

    white_peace_possible = no

    cost = {
    }

    target_titles = all
    target_title_tier = all

    on_declaration = {
        on_declared_war = yes
    }

    should_invalidate = {
        scope:defender = {
            OR = {
                # Is vassal for some reason
                is_independent_ruler = no
                # Is landless nobody for some reason
                NOR = {
                    is_landed = yes
                    is_landless_ruler = yes
                }
            }
        }
    }

    on_invalidated_desc = {
        desc = selian_defiance_war_invalidated_message
    }
    
    on_invalidated = {
        every_war_attacker = {
            add_prestige = medium_prestige_gain
            add_piety = medium_piety_gain
        }
        scope:defender = {
            add_prestige = medium_prestige_gain
        }
    }

    on_victory_desc = {
        first_valid = {
            triggered_desc = {
                trigger = { scope:defender = { is_local_player = yes } }
                desc = selian_defiance_loses_defender
            }
            triggered_desc = {
                trigger = { scope:attacker = { is_local_player = yes } }
                desc = selian_defiance_loses_attacker
            }
            desc = selian_defiance_loses_other
        }
    }

    # ATTACKERS WIN (Selian Restoration Fails)
    on_victory = {
        # Perspective events for the war's outcome, mirroring HAAO's
        # disobedient_war_cb post-war events (haao_hegemony.0101/0102).
        # Looked up first, before the defender's realm below is dismantled.
        hidden_effect = {
            scope:attacker = {
                random_character_war = {
                    limit = { using_cb = selian_defiance_cb }
                    every_war_attacker = { add_to_list = nars_defiance_victors }
                    every_war_defender = { add_to_list = nars_defiance_defenders }
                }
            }
            every_player = {
                limit = { is_in_list = nars_defiance_victors }
                trigger_event = { id = nars_hegemony.0101 days = 2 }
            }
            # Defender-side allies (not the aspirant themselves -- they get their
            # own dedicated nars_hegemony.0106 cutscene below)
            every_player = {
                limit = {
                    is_in_list = nars_defiance_defenders
                    NOT = { this = scope:defender }
                }
                trigger_event = { id = nars_hegemony.0102 days = 2 }
            }
            scope:defender = {
                trigger_event = { id = nars_hegemony.0106 days = 2 }
            }
        }

        # Legitimacy for attackers
        add_legitimacy_attacker_victory_effect = yes

        scope:defender = {            
            primary_title = {
                destroy_title = yes
            }
            
            add_legitimacy = monumental_legitimacy_loss
            remove_short_term_gold = ninety_percent_gold_value
            add_prestige = negative_seventy_five_percent_prestige_value
            add_prestige_level = -1

            create_title_and_vassal_change = {
                type = independency
                save_scope_as = defender_realm_collapse
                add_claim_on_loss = no
            }

            every_vassal = {
                limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_county }
                becomes_independent = { change = scope:defender_realm_collapse }
            }

            resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:defender_realm_collapse
        }

        every_war_attacker = {
            add_short_term_gold = medium_gold_value
        }

        scope:attacker = {
            rightfully_imprison_character_less_verbose_effect = { 
                TARGET = scope:defender
                IMPRISONER = scope:attacker
            }
            add_prestige_experience = {
                value = major_prestige_value
            }
            show_pow_release_message_effect = yes
        }

        # Allies on both sides get fame
        modify_allies_of_participants_fame_values = {
            WINNER = scope:attacker
            FAME_BASE = major_prestige_value
            IS_RELIGIOUS_WAR = no
            WINNER_ALLY_FAME_SCALE = 10
            LOSER_ALLY_FAME_SCALE = 1
        }

        laamp_as_mercenary_payout_tooltip_effect = yes
        add_truce_attacker_victory_effect = yes
    }

    on_white_peace_desc = {}

    on_defeat_desc = {
        first_valid = {
            triggered_desc = {
                trigger = { scope:defender = { is_local_player = yes } }
                desc = selian_defiance_wins_defender
            }
            desc = selian_defiance_wins_other
        }
    }

    # DEFENDER WINS (Attackers lose and their realms shatter)
    on_defeat = {
        # Perspective events for the coalition's defeat, mirroring HAAO's
        # disobedient_war_cb post-war events (haao_hegemony.0103/0104). The
        # aspirant's own victory perspective is nars_hegemony.0105, triggered
        # from scope:defender's effects below (replaces nars_hegemon.0001
        # as the entry point -- 0105 itself still chains into nars_hegemon.0001
        # for the reward/flavor beat).
        hidden_effect = {
            scope:defender = {
                random_character_war = {
                    limit = { using_cb = selian_defiance_cb }
                    every_war_attacker = { add_to_list = nars_defiance_losers }
                    every_war_defender = { add_to_list = nars_defiance_winners }
                }
            }
            every_player = {
                limit = { is_in_list = nars_defiance_losers }
                trigger_event = { id = nars_hegemony.0103 days = 2 }
            }
            # Defender-side allies (not the aspirant themselves -- they get
            # nars_hegemony.0105 below)
            every_player = {
                limit = {
                    is_in_list = nars_defiance_winners
                    NOT = { this = scope:defender }
                }
                trigger_event = { id = nars_hegemony.0104 days = 2 }
            }
        }

        add_to_global_variable_list = {
            name = unavailable_unique_decisions
            target = flag:flag_decision_narsokratia
        }

        scope:attacker = {
            add_legitimacy = medium_legitimacy_loss
            add_to_list = anti_nars_attackers
            random_character_war = {
				limit = {
					using_cb = selian_defiance_cb
				}
                add_to_list = anti_nars_attackers
            }
        }
        
        # Truce effects
        add_truce_attacker_defeat_effect = yes

        scope:defender = {
            add_legitimacy = monumental_legitimacy_gain
            add_prestige = monumental_prestige_gain
            
            # Delete all truces with attackers so you can immediately counter-attack
            hidden_effect = {
                every_truce_target = {
                    scope:defender = { cancel_truce_one_way = prev }
                }
            }
            
            trigger_event = {
                id = nars_hegemony.0105
                days = 1
            } 
        }

        modify_allies_of_participants_fame_values = {
            WINNER = scope:defender
            FAME_BASE = major_prestige_value
            IS_RELIGIOUS_WAR = no
            WINNER_ALLY_FAME_SCALE = 10
            LOSER_ALLY_FAME_SCALE = 1
        }

        scope:attacker = {
            every_in_list = {
			    list = anti_nars_attackers
                every_war_attacker = {
					create_title_and_vassal_change = {
                        type = independency
                        save_scope_as = attacker_realm_collapse
                        add_claim_on_loss = no
                    }

                    every_vassal = {
                        limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_county }
                        becomes_independent = { change = scope:attacker_realm_collapse }
                    }

                    resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:attacker_realm_collapse
				}
            }
        }

        laamp_as_mercenary_payout_tooltip_effect = yes
    }

    on_primary_attacker_death = inherit
    on_primary_defender_death = inherit
    check_defender_inheritance_validity = yes
    transfer_behavior = transfer

    attacker_allies_inherit = yes
    defender_allies_inherit = yes

    war_name = "SELIAN_DEFIANCE_WAR_NAME"
    my_war_name = "SELIAN_DEFIANCE_WAR_NAME_MY"
    war_name_base = "SELIAN_DEFIANCE_WAR_NAME_BASE"
    cb_name = "SELIAN_DEFIANCE_CB_NAME"
    interface_priority = 60

	type = heritage
	is_shown = {
		heritage_is_shown_trigger = {
			HERITAGE = heritage_nars
		}
	}
	audio_parameter = selian

language_nars = {
	type = language
	is_shown = {
		language_is_shown_trigger = {
			LANGUAGE = language_nars
		}
		scope:character = { is_selian = yes }
	}
	ai_will_do = {
		value = 10
		if = {
			limit = { has_cultural_pillar = language_nars }
			multiply = 100
		}
	}
	
	color = greek
}
﻿# Nars Culture Traditions should have 3 default tradition and 1 unique to the culture.

tradition_nars_merchant = {
    category = societal
    layers = { 0 = learning 1 = mena3 4 = jirga.dds }
        is_shown = {
            OR = {
                has_cultural_pillar = heritage_nars
            }
	}
	can_pick = {
		OR = {
			has_cultural_pillar = heritage_nars
            #make it so that the requirement is subsume the x faction, e.g. Subsume the Patres
		}
    }
   parameters = {
        trade_ports_stationed_maa_bonus = yes
        trade_ports_give_control_growth = yes
        next_level_trade_ports = yes
        coastal_agriculture_building_bonuses = yes
        maritime_mercantilism_coastal_holdings = yes
        culture_can_raid_over_land_even_if_feudal_no_restrictions = yes
        culture_can_raid_at_sea_even_if_feudal_no_restrictions = yes
        can_travel_along_rivers = yes
    }
    character_modifier = {
        sea_travel_danger = medium_sea_danger_reduction
        coastal_sea_travel_danger = medium_sea_danger_reduction
        movement_speed = 0.2
        diplomatic_range_mult = 0.2
    }
}

tradition_nars_agriculture = { # Agrarian & Artisan
    category = societal
    layers = { 0 = learning 1 = mena3 4 = fierce_independence.dds }
    is_shown = {
		has_cultural_tradition = tradition_nars_merchant
	}
	can_pick = {
		OR = {
			has_cultural_pillar = heritage_nars
            #make it so that the requirement is subsume the x faction, e.g. Subsume the Patres
		}
    }
    parameters = { 
        unlock_nars_t1 = yes
        martial_education_more_valued = yes
        prowess_from_martial_education = yes
        strong_traits_more_valued = yes
        strong_traits_more_common = yes
        diligent_trait_more_common = yes
        hard_working_traits_more_valued = yes
        gain_gold_from_battles = yes
        has_access_to_trials_by_combat = yes
        can_duel_rivals = yes
    }
    character_modifier = { 
        character_travel_speed_mult = 0.125
        knight_effectiveness_mult = 0.25
    }
}

tradition_nars_science = { 
    category = societal
    layers = { 0 = learning 1 = mena3 4 = beacon_of_learning.dds }
    is_shown = {
		has_cultural_tradition = tradition_nars_merchant

    parameters = {

        less_likely_to_culture_convert = yes
        harder_to_hybridize = yes
        reduced_marriage_acceptance_outside_culture = yes
		reduced_marriage_acceptance_distant_realms = yes
		ai_doesnt_marry_outside_culture = yes
        better_disease_resistance = yes
        tells_stories = yes
    }
    character_modifier = {
        monthly_lifestyle_xp_gain_mult = 0.15
        opinion_of_different_culture = -150
    }
}

tradition_nars_builder = { 
    category = societal
    layers = { 0 = learning 1 = mena3 4 = mountain.dds }
    is_shown = {
		OR = {
			has_cultural_pillar = heritage_nars
		}
	}
	can_pick = {
		OR = {
			has_cultural_pillar = heritage_nars
            #make it so that the requirement is subsume the x faction, e.g. Subsume the Patres
		}
    }
    parameters = {
        next_level_w_mills = yes
        rice_cultivators_can_construct_paddy_fields_in_hills_and_jungle = yes
        farm_estates_pastures_unlock = yes
        development_gain_on_building_complete = yes
        renown_from_temple_construction = yes
        landing_house_members_gives_renown = yes
        can_recruit_prisoners_easily = yes
    }
    character_modifier = {

					is_barony_valid_for_brothel_complex = {
						HOLDER = root
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = establish_brothel_realm_limit_tt
			top_liege = {
				count_sub_realm_county = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							any_in_de_jure_hierarchy = {
								title_province = {
									has_building_or_higher = brothel_complex_01
								}
							}
							has_county_modifier = nars_construction_modifier
						}
					}
					count < 3
				}
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = establish_brothel_cooldown_tt
				has_character_flag = establish_brothel_cooldown
			}
		}
	}

	effect = {
		custom_tooltip = decision_establish_brothel_effect_tooltip
		open_view_data = {
			view = decision_detail
			data = decision:pick_brothel_barony
			player = root
		}
	}
	
	ai_check_interval = 13
	ai_potential = {
		is_ruler = yes
		any_held_county = {
			any_in_de_jure_hierarchy = {
                is_barony_valid_for_brothel_complex = {
					HOLDER = root
				}
			}
		}
	}
	ai_will_do = {
        base = 100
        modifier = {
			add = -50
			any_held_county = {
				OR = {
					any_in_de_jure_hierarchy = {
						title_province = {
							has_building_or_higher = brothel_complex_01
						}
					}
					has_county_modifier = nars_construction_modifier
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
