decision_narsokratia = {
    picture = {
        reference = "gfx/interface/illustrations/decisions/nars_decision_basileus.dds"
    }
    desc = decision_narsokratia_desc
    selection_tooltip = decision_narsokratia_tooltip
    decision_group_type = oaths
    sort_order = 1000

	cooldown = { months = 6 }

    is_shown = {
        is_independent_ruler = yes
		is_landed = yes
        is_ethnically_selian = yes
        is_selian = yes
        NOT = {
			is_target_in_global_variable_list = {
				name = unavailable_unique_decisions
				target = flag:flag_decision_narsokratia
			}
		}
    }

    is_valid = {
		custom_description = {
			text = found_hegemony_decision_eight_kingdoms_held
			any_held_title = {
				count >= 7
				title_tier = kingdom
			}
		}
        custom_tooltip = {
			text = decision_narsokratia_req_tt
			any_character_artifact = { has_variable = can_be_used_for_narsokratia }
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = you_are_the_culture_head
			culture = {
				culture_head = root
			}
		}
        prestige_level >= 5
       	custom_tooltip = { text = decision_narsokratia_enough_high_level_knights any_knight = { count >= 12 prowess >= 18 }  }
    }

    is_valid_showing_failures_only = {
        is_landed = yes
        is_capable_adult = yes
        is_imprisoned = no
        OR = { is_archaic = yes is_selian = yes }
    }
    
    cost = { prestige = 5000 }
    
    effect = {
		custom_tooltip = decision_nars_hegemony_tt

		send_interface_message = {
			type = event_prowess_good_desc
			title = narsokratia_declared_toast_title
			desc = narsokratia_declared_toast_desc
			left_icon = root
		}

		hidden_effect = {
			add_character_modifier = {
				modifier = nars_nikator_aspirant_modifier
				years = 5
			}
			spawn_army = {
				men_at_arms = { type = nars_t1 stacks = 15 }
				levies = 2750
				inheritable = no
				uses_supply = no
				location = root.capital_province
				name = event_troop_default_name
			}
		}

		hidden_effect = {
			every_neighboring_and_across_water_top_liege_realm_owner = {
				limit = {
					is_independent_ruler = yes
					NOT = { has_alliance = root }
					NOT = { has_truce = root }
				}
				add_to_list = selian_coalition_members
			}

			trigger_event = nars_hegemony.0001
		}
	}
        
    ai_potential = { always = no }
}

@maa_high_maintenance_cost = 5.0
@cultural_maa_extra_ai_score = 60 # Equivalent to having 6 extra regiments beyond what the code scoring would indicate (see NEGATIVE_SCORE_PER_EXISTING_REGIMENT)

# Must be synced between files with the values located at "# Provisions Costs #".
@provisions_cost_infantry_cheap = 3
@provisions_cost_infantry_moderate = 7
@provisions_cost_infantry_expensive = 12
@provisions_cost_infantry_bankrupting = 15

@provisions_cost_special_cheap = 6
@provisions_cost_special_moderate = 12

nars_t1 = {
    type = heavy_infantry

    can_recruit = {
	    culture = { has_cultural_parameter = unlock_nars_t1 }
        NOT = {
			culture = { has_cultural_parameter = strength_in_numbers_heavy_maa_ban }
		}
        is_selian = yes
    }

    damage = 45
    toughness = 30
    pursuit = 10
    screen = 15

    terrain_bonus = {
        plains = { damage = 25 toughness = 10 }
        mountains = { damage = 25 toughness = 10 }
        terraced_hills = { damage = 25 toughness = 10 }
        hills = { damage = 50 toughness = 20 }
        forest = { damage = -25 toughness = -10 }
        wetlands = { damage = -25 toughness = -10 }
    }
	
    mercenary_fallback = no
    holy_order_fallback = no
    
	counters = {
		pikemen = 1
		peasant_militia = 2
		heavy_cavalry = 1
        archers = 0.5
	}

	winter_bonus = {
		normal_winter = { toughness = -5 screen = -5 }
		harsh_winter = { toughness = -5 screen = -5 }
	}

    buy_cost = { gold = schiltron_recruitment_cost }
	low_maintenance_cost = { gold = schiltron_low_maint_cost }
	high_maintenance_cost = { gold = schiltron_high_maint_cost }
	provision_cost = @provisions_cost_infantry_moderate
    
    stack = 100
   	ai_quality = {
        value = culture_ai_weight_heavy_infantry
        add = counter_synergy_ai_weight_heavy_infantry
		add = 40
    }
    icon = nars_t1
}

}﻿# --- 1. CORE SETUP EFFECT ---
nars_one_time_maintenance_on_action = {
    trigger = {
        NOT = { has_character_flag = selian_initialized }
		is_alive = yes
		is_ethnically_selian = yes
    }
    effect = {
        add_character_flag = selian_initialized
		nars_apply_setup_effect = yes
		nars_naturalis_set_tier_xp_effect = yes
    }
}

# --- 1B. YEARLY TIER SYNC (UNGATED) ---
# nars_one_time_maintenance_on_action above only ever fires ONCE per
# character (it is gated behind selian_initialized), so it cannot keep
# nars_naturalis' tier in sync after that first firing. Education rank
# (and therefore the trait's tier) can still change well after game start
# and after adulthood, so this runs every year, for as long as the
# character holds the trait, completely independent of that flag.
nars_naturalis_yearly_sync_on_action = {
    trigger = {
        is_alive = yes
        has_trait = nars_naturalis
    }
    effect = {
        nars_naturalis_set_tier_xp_effect = yes
    }
}

# --- 2. STARTUP ON_ACTIONS ---
on_game_start = {
    on_actions = {
        nars_global_world_setup_on_action
        delay = { days = { 3 9 } }
        nars_global_world_setup_on_action
    }
}

on_game_start_after_lobby = {
    on_actions = { 
        nars_intro_on_start
    }
}

# --- 3. CHARACTER CREATION HOOKS ---
# This covers every possible way a character can enter the game
on_character_created = { 
    effect = { nars_apply_setup_effect = yes } 
}
on_pool_character_created = { 
    effect = { nars_apply_setup_effect = yes }
}
on_birth_child = { 
    effect = { nars_apply_setup_effect = yes } 
}
on_ruler_designer_creation = { 
    effect = { nars_apply_setup_effect = yes } 
}

# --- 4. GLOBAL SETUP ACTIONS ---
nars_global_world_setup_on_action = {
    effect = {
        # One single loop for all character traits and XP syncing at game start
        every_living_character = {
            if = {
                limit = { 
                    OR = {
                        is_ethnically_selian = yes
                        has_culture = culture:selian
                    }
                    NOT = { has_trait = nars_naturalis }
                }
                add_trait = nars_naturalis
                nars_naturalis_set_tier_xp_effect = yes
            }
            else_if = {
                limit = { has_trait = nars_naturalis }
                nars_naturalis_set_tier_xp_effect = yes
            }
        }
        
        every_domicile = {
            limit = {
                exists = owner
                owner = {
                    is_alive = yes
                    is_landless_adventurer = yes
                    is_ethnically_selian = yes
                }
            }
            nars_laamp_camp_setup_effect = yes
        }
    }
}


# --- 5. MAINTENANCE HOOKS ---
on_guest_arrived_from_pool = { on_actions = { nars_one_time_maintenance_on_action } }
on_16th_birthday = { on_actions = { nars_one_time_maintenance_on_action } }
on_birthday = { on_actions = { nars_one_time_maintenance_on_action } }
on_join_court = { on_actions = { nars_one_time_maintenance_on_action } }
random_yearly_everyone_pulse = { on_actions = { nars_one_time_maintenance_on_action nars_naturalis_yearly_sync_on_action } }
on_title_gain = { events = { delay = { days = 3 } nars_maintenance.0002 } }

on_trait_added = {
    effect = {
        if = {
            limit = { has_trait = nars_naturalis }
            nars_naturalis_set_tier_xp_effect = yes
        }
    }
}


# --- 6. INTRO EVENTS ---
nars_intro_on_start = {
    effect = {
        every_ruler = {
            limit = {
                is_alive = yes
                is_selian = yes
                is_independent_ruler = yes
            }
            
            add_gold = { 400 1000 }
            add_prestige = { 600 1200 }
            add_piety = { 600 1200 }
            add_prestige_level = 2
            add_piety_level = 1
            nars_maa_setup_effect = yes
            
            if = {
                limit = { is_landed = yes } 
                selian_settle_county_effect = yes
            }
        }
        # 1. Player-Specific Intro Events
        every_player = {
            limit = {
                is_alive = yes
                is_selian = yes
            }
            trigger_event = nars_intro.1001
        }
        if = {
            limit = {
                any_player = {
                    is_alive = yes
                    is_selian = no
                }
            }
            random_player = {
                limit = {
                    is_alive = yes
                    is_selian = no
                }
                trigger_event = nars_intro.0001
            }
        }
        else = {
            random_ruler = {
                limit = {
                    is_alive = yes
                    is_ai = yes
                }
                trigger_event = nars_intro.0001
            }
        }
    } 
}

    base_maximum_success = 90

        }
    }

    on_start = {
        set_variable = {
            name = apply_countermeasures
            value = flag:indirect
        }
    }

    on_phase_completed = {
        save_scope_as = scheme
        scheme_owner = {
            save_scope_as = owner
            random_owned_story = {
                limit = { story_type = story_produce_amphora }
                save_scope_as = amphora_story
            }
        }

        random = {
            chance = scope:scheme.scheme_success_chance
            save_scope_value_as = { name = scheme_successful value = yes }
        }

        scope:owner = {
            if = {
                limit = { exists = scope:scheme_successful }
                trigger_event = nars_amphora.0001
                if = {
                    limit = { exists = scope:amphora_story }
                    scope:amphora_story = {
                        change_variable = {
                            name = produce_amphora_success
                            add = 1
                        }
                    }
                    scope:owner = {
                        set_variable = {
                            name = produce_amphora_success
                            value = scope:amphora_story.var:produce_amphora_success
                        }
                    }
                    if = {
                        limit = {
                            scope:owner = {
                                var:produce_amphora_success >= 5
                            }
                        }
                        scope:owner = {
                            trigger_event = nars_amphora.9000
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            else = {
                trigger_event = { on_action = produce_amphora_scheme_failure }
            }
        }
    }

    on_invalidated = {
        scheme_owner = {
            save_scope_as = owner
            random_owned_story = {
                limit = { story_type = story_produce_amphora }
                save_scope_as = amphora_story
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { exists = scope:amphora_story }
            scope:amphora_story = {
                end_story = yes
            }
        }
    }
}﻿piously_devote_scheme = {
	# Basic Setup
	skill = learning
	desc = "piously_devote_scheme_desc_general"
	success_desc = "piously_devote_scheme_SCHEME_SUCCESS_DESC"
	icon =	icon_sacred
	illustration = "gfx/interface/illustrations/event_scenes/nars_faith.dds"
	category = personal
	target_type = character

	is_secret = no
	is_basic = yes
	
	hide_target_name = yes
	freeze_scheme_when_traveling = yes

	# Parameters
	speed_per_skill_point = -2
	spymaster_speed_per_skill_point = 0	
	uses_resistance = no
	base_progress_goal = 225
	base_maximum_success = 95
	minimum_success = 5

	# Core Triggers
	allow = {
		age >= 12
		is_imprisoned = no
		NOT = { has_trait = incapable }
	}

	valid = {
		scope:target = scope:owner
		NOT = {
			has_character_modifier = partially_mute_modifier
		}
		is_imprisoned = no
		NOT = { has_trait = incapable }
	}

	odds_prediction = {
		add = odds_nars_scheme_misc_value
		min = 0
	}

	# Base Chances
	base_success_chance = {
		base = 50
		# Skill
		modifier = {
			add = scope:owner.learning
			desc = "SCHEME_MY_LEARNING"
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -10
			desc = SCHEME_OWNER_LANDED
			scope:owner = { is_landed = yes }
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 10
			desc = SCHEME_OWNER_UNLANDED
			scope:owner = { is_landed = no }
		}
		# Personality traits
		modifier = {
			add = -10
			desc = SCHEME_OWNER_LAZY
			scope:owner = { has_trait = lazy }
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -30
			desc = SCHEME_OWNER_BURDENED
			scope:owner = { has_trait = burdened }
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -20
			desc = SCHEME_OWNER_CYNICAL
			scope:owner = { has_trait = cynical }
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 10
			desc = SCHEME_OWNER_DILIGENT
			scope:owner = { has_trait = diligent }
		}
		modifier = {
			add = -10
			desc = SCHEME_OWNER_IMPATIENT
			scope:owner = { has_trait = impatient }
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 10
			desc = SCHEME_OWNER_STUBBORN
			scope:owner = { has_trait = stubborn }
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 10
			desc = SCHEME_OWNER_ZEALOUS
			scope:owner = { has_trait = zealous }
		}
	}

	# On Actions
	on_phase_completed = {
		# Save scopes
		save_scope_as = scheme
		scheme_target_character = { save_scope_as = target }
		scheme_owner = { save_scope_as = owner }
		# Let's see if we made progress
		random = {
			chance = scope:scheme.scheme_success_chance
			save_scope_value_as = {
				name = scheme_successful
				value = yes
			}
		}
		# HAAO - personal devotion level 3+ towards the tutelary deity: a bad show is half as likely. A failed roll gets
		# a second, even chance (scripted_triggers/HAAO_cultus_triggers.txt)
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { exists = scope:scheme_successful }
				scope:owner = { deity_devotion_softens_bad_show = yes }
			}
			random = {
				chance = 50
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = scheme_successful
					value = yes
				}
			}
		}
		# Determine the outcome
		scope:owner = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = scope:scheme_successful }
				trigger_event = { on_action = piously_devote_scheme_success }
			}
			else = {
				trigger_event = { on_action = piously_devote_scheme_failure }
			}
		}
		reset_scheme_progress = yes
	}

	## Invalidation
	on_invalidated = {
	}
}

}﻿sponsor_meithios_scheme = {
	skill = learning
	desc = "sponsor_meithios_scheme_desc_general"
	success_desc = "sponsor_meithios_scheme_SUCCESS_DESC"
	icon = icon_sacred
	illustration = "gfx/interface/illustrations/event_scenes/nars_faith.dds"
	category = personal
	target_type = character

	is_secret = no
	is_basic = yes

	hide_target_name = yes

	speed_per_skill_point = 0
	spymaster_speed_per_skill_point = 0
	uses_resistance = no
	base_progress_goal = 100

	allow = {
		age >= 12
		is_imprisoned = no
		NOT = { has_trait = incapable }
	}

	valid = {
		scope:target = scope:owner
		is_imprisoned = no
		NOT = { has_trait = incapable }
		scope:owner = {
			nars_faith_scheme_has_targets_trigger = yes
		}
	}

	base_success_chance = {
		base = 100
	}

	on_start = {
		set_variable = {
			name = apply_countermeasures
			value = flag:indirect
		}
	}

	on_phase_completed = {
		save_scope_as = scheme
		scheme_owner = {
			save_scope_as = owner
			random_owned_story = {
				limit = { story_type = story_sponsor_meithios }
				save_scope_as = sponsor_meithios_story
			}
		}

		scope:owner = {
			trigger_event = nars_faith.0001
			if = {
				limit = { exists = scope:sponsor_meithios_story }
				scope:sponsor_meithios_story = {
					change_variable = {
						name = sponsor_meithios_success
						add = 1
					}
				}
				scope:owner = {
					set_variable = {
						name = sponsor_meithios_success
						value = scope:sponsor_meithios_story.var:sponsor_meithios_success
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:owner = {
							var:sponsor_meithios_success >= 3
						}
					}
					scope:scheme = { end_scheme = yes }
					scope:sponsor_meithios_story = { end_story = yes }
					scope:owner = {
						add_character_flag = {
							flag = cooldown_meithios_flag
							years = 10
						}
					}
					save_scope_value_as = {
						name = sponsor_meithios_scheme_ended
						value = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = scope:sponsor_meithios_scheme_ended } }
			reset_scheme_progress = yes
		}
	}

	on_invalidated = {
		scheme_owner = {
			save_scope_as = owner
			random_owned_story = {
				limit = { story_type = story_sponsor_meithios }
				save_scope_as = sponsor_meithios_story
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:sponsor_meithios_story }
			scope:sponsor_meithios_story = {
				end_story = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

selian_minor_county_effect = {
	every_realm_county = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { faith = root.faith }
		}
		random = {
			chance = 25
			set_county_faith = root.faith
			set_county_rite = root.rite
            change_county_control = minor_county_control_loss
		}
	}
	every_realm_county = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { culture = culture:selian }
		}
		random = {
			chance = 25
			set_county_culture = culture:selian
            change_county_control = minor_county_control_loss
		}
	}
}

selian_major_county_effect = {
	every_realm_county = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { faith = root.faith }
		}
		random = {
			chance = 75
			set_county_faith = root.faith
			set_county_rite = root.rite
            change_county_control = minor_county_control_loss
		}
	}
	every_realm_county = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { culture = culture:selian }
		}
		random = {
			chance = 75
			set_county_culture = culture:selian
            change_county_control = minor_county_control_loss
		}
	}
}

selian_settle_county_effect = {
    # Save the character triggering the effect so we can reference them safely
    save_scope_as = selian_ruler
    
    every_sub_realm_county = {
        limit = {
            # Check if the county belongs to a Duchy held by the ruler, 
            # OR if it belongs to the de jure Duchy of the ruler's capital.
            duchy = {
                OR = {
                    holder = scope:selian_ruler
                    this = scope:selian_ruler.capital_county.duchy
                }
            }
            
            # Only target the county if it actually needs a conversion
            OR = {
                NOT = { faith = root.faith }
                NOT = { culture = culture:selian }
            }
        }
        
        # Convert Faith if needed
        if = {
            limit = {
                NOT = { faith = root.faith }
            }
            set_county_faith = root.faith
            set_county_rite = root.rite
        }
        
        # Convert Culture if needed
        if = {
            limit = {
                NOT = { culture = culture:selian }
            }
            set_county_culture = culture:selian
        }
        
        # Apply the control loss once
        change_county_control = major_county_control_loss
    }
}

selian_diplomacy_entreaty_effect = {
    custom_tooltip = selian_diplomacy.tt
    
    hidden_effect = {
        # Dynamically identify the other two siblings based on the root character's modifier
        if = {
            limit = { has_character_modifier = eleios_modifier }
            father = {
                random_child = { limit = { has_character_modifier = ilione_modifier } save_scope_as = target_1 }
                random_child = { limit = { has_character_modifier = strato_modifier } save_scope_as = target_2 }
            }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { has_character_modifier = ilione_modifier }
            father = {
                random_child = { limit = { has_character_modifier = eleios_modifier } save_scope_as = target_1 }
                random_child = { limit = { has_character_modifier = strato_modifier } save_scope_as = target_2 }
            }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { has_character_modifier = strato_modifier }
            father = {
                random_child = { limit = { has_character_modifier = eleios_modifier } save_scope_as = target_1 }
                random_child = { limit = { has_character_modifier = ilione_modifier } save_scope_as = target_2 }
            }
        }
        
        random_list = {
            20 = {
                modifier = {
                    add = 10
                    scope:target_1 = { opinion: { target = root value >= 50 } }
                    scope:target_2 = { opinion: { target = root value >= 50 } }
                }
                
                send_interface_message = {
                    title = selian_diplomacy_both_join_toast_t
                    desc = selian_diplomacy_both_join_toast_tt
                    type = event_diplomacy_good_with_text
                    left_icon = scope:target_1
                    right_icon = scope:target_2
                }
                
                scope:target_1 = {
                    hidden_effect = { laamp_clear_domicile_buildings_liquidator_effect = yes }
                    every_courtier = { add_to_list = target_1_followers_list }
                    destroy_laamp_effect = { ADVENTURER = this }
                    remove_trait = adventurer
                    
                    root = { 
                        add_courtier = scope:target_1 
                        return_to_court = yes 
                    }
                    
                    hidden_effect = {
                        every_in_list = {
                            list = target_1_followers_list
                            limit = { NOT = { liege_or_court_owner = root } }
                            root = { add_courtier = prev }
                            remove_trait = adventurer_follower
                        }
                    }
                }
                
                scope:target_2 = {
                    hidden_effect = { laamp_clear_domicile_buildings_liquidator_effect = yes }
                    every_courtier = { add_to_list = target_2_followers_list }
                    destroy_laamp_effect = { ADVENTURER = this }
                    remove_trait = adventurer
                    
                    root = { 
                        add_courtier = scope:target_2 
                        return_to_court = yes 
                    }
                    
                    hidden_effect = {
                        every_in_list = {
                            list = target_2_followers_list
                            limit = { NOT = { liege_or_court_owner = root } }
                            root = { add_courtier = prev }
                            remove_trait = adventurer_follower
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            25 = {
                send_interface_message = {
                    title = selian_diplomacy_half_joins_toast_t
                    type = event_diplomacy_good_with_text
                    left_icon = scope:target_1
                    right_icon = scope:target_2
                    desc = selian_diplomacy_target_1_joins_toast_tt
                }
                scope:target_1 = {
                    hidden_effect = { laamp_clear_domicile_buildings_liquidator_effect = yes }
                    every_courtier = { add_to_list = target_1_followers_list }
                    destroy_laamp_effect = { ADVENTURER = this }
                    remove_trait = adventurer
                    
                    root = { 
                        add_courtier = scope:target_1 
                        return_to_court = yes 
                    }
                    
                    hidden_effect = {
                        every_in_list = {
                            list = target_1_followers_list
                            limit = { NOT = { liege_or_court_owner = root } }
                            root = { add_courtier = prev }
                            remove_trait = adventurer_follower
                        }
                    }
                }
                scope:target_2 = {
                    add_opinion = {
                        target = root
                        modifier = disapproving_opinion
                    }
                    set_relation_rival = {
                        reason = rival_claimant_fight
                        target = root
                    } 
                }
            }
            25 = {
                send_interface_message = {
                    title = selian_diplomacy_half_joins_toast_t
                    type = event_diplomacy_good_with_text
                    left_icon = scope:target_1
                    right_icon = scope:target_2
                    desc = selian_diplomacy_target_2_joins_toast_tt
                }

                scope:target_1 = {
                    add_opinion = {
                        target = root
                        modifier = disapproving_opinion
                    }
                    set_relation_rival = {
                        reason = rival_claimant_fight
                        target = root
                    } 
                }
                scope:target_2 = {
                    hidden_effect = { laamp_clear_domicile_buildings_liquidator_effect = yes }
                    every_courtier = { add_to_list = target_2_followers_list }
                    destroy_laamp_effect = { ADVENTURER = this }
                    remove_trait = adventurer
                    
                    root = { 
                        add_courtier = scope:target_2 
                        return_to_court = yes 
                    }
                    
                    hidden_effect = {
                        every_in_list = {
                            list = target_2_followers_list
                            limit = { NOT = { liege_or_court_owner = root } }
                            root = { add_courtier = prev }
                            remove_trait = adventurer_follower
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            30 = {
                send_interface_message = {
                    title = selian_diplomacy_neither_join_toast_t
                    type = event_diplomacy_bad_with_text
                    left_icon = scope:target_1
                    right_icon = scope:target_2
                    desc = selian_diplomacy_neither_join_toast_tt
                }
                scope:target_1 = {
                    add_opinion = {
                        target = root

                        set_relation_rival = {
                            reason = rival_claimant_fight
                            target = root
                        }

                }
                scope:target_2 = {

                        target = root
                        modifier = disapproving_opinion
                        set_relation_rival = {
                            reason = rival_claimant_fight
                            target = root
                        }

                }
            }
        }
    }
}﻿local_assimilate_selian_traditions_effect = {
	save_scope_as = converting_character
	
	$OLD_CULTURE$ = { save_scope_as = old_culture }
	$NEW_CULTURE$ = { save_scope_as = new_culture }
	$OLD_FAITH$ = { save_scope_as = old_faith }
	$NEW_FAITH$ = { save_scope_as = new_faith }
	$CONVERTER$ = { save_scope_as = converter }

	add_character_modifier = {
		modifier = embraced_selian_traditions_modifier
	}
	
	if = {
		limit = {
			dynasty ?= {
				NOT = { has_dynasty_modifier = settled_selian_dynasty_modifier }
			}
		}
		dynasty = {
			add_dynasty_modifier = { modifier = settled_selian_dynasty_modifier years = 80 }
		}
	}
	
	set_culture = scope:new_culture
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { faith = scope:new_faith } }
		hidden_effect = { bastard_to_wild_oat_conversion_assimilation_effect = yes }
		set_character_faith = scope:new_faith
	}
	
	if = {
		limit = { is_ai = yes }
		add_character_flag = converted_culture_this_lifetime_ai
	}
	else = { add_character_flag = converted_culture_this_lifetime }

	every_close_family_member = {
		limit = { is_courtier_of = scope:converter }
		set_culture = scope:new_culture
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { faith = scope:new_faith } }
			hidden_effect = { bastard_to_wild_oat_conversion_assimilation_effect = yes }
			set_character_faith = scope:new_faith
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			add_character_modifier = { modifier = embraced_selian_traditions_modifier }
		}
		add_to_temporary_list = notified_of_culture_change
	}
	
	every_consort = {
		limit = {
			is_ruler = no
			NOT = { is_in_list = notified_of_culture_change }
		}
		set_culture = scope:new_culture
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { faith = scope:new_faith } }
			hidden_effect = { bastard_to_wild_oat_conversion_assimilation_effect = yes }
			set_character_faith = scope:new_faith
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			add_character_modifier = { modifier = embraced_selian_traditions_modifier }
		}
		add_to_temporary_list = notified_of_culture_change
	}

	every_vassal = {
		limit = { NOT = { is_in_list = notified_of_culture_change } }
		
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = embraced_selian_traditions_modifier }
		
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					dynasty ?= {
						NOT = { has_dynasty_modifier = settled_selian_dynasty_modifier }
					}
				}
				dynasty = {
					add_dynasty_modifier = { modifier = settled_selian_dynasty_modifier years = 80 }
				}
			}
		}
		
		set_culture = scope:new_culture
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { faith = scope:new_faith } }
			hidden_effect = { bastard_to_wild_oat_conversion_assimilation_effect = yes }
			set_character_faith = scope:new_faith
		}

		hidden_effect = {
			every_close_family_member = {
				limit = { is_courtier_of = prev }
				set_culture = scope:new_culture
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { faith = scope:new_faith } }
					hidden_effect = { bastard_to_wild_oat_conversion_assimilation_effect = yes }
					set_character_faith = scope:new_faith
				}
				hidden_effect = { add_character_modifier = { modifier = embraced_selian_traditions_modifier } }
				add_to_temporary_list = notified_of_culture_change
			}
			every_consort = {
				limit = {
					is_ruler = no
					NOT = { is_in_list = notified_of_culture_change }
				}
				set_culture = scope:new_culture
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { faith = scope:new_faith } }
					hidden_effect = { bastard_to_wild_oat_conversion_assimilation_effect = yes }
					set_character_faith = scope:new_faith
				}
				hidden_effect = { add_character_modifier = { modifier = embraced_selian_traditions_modifier } }
				add_to_temporary_list = notified_of_culture_change
			}
		}

		add_to_temporary_list = notified_of_culture_change
	}

	hidden_effect = {
		every_player = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_consort_of = scope:converting_character
					liege ?= scope:converting_character
					AND = {
						exists = scope:converting_character.liege
						this = scope:converting_character.liege
					}
				}
				NOT = { is_in_list = notified_of_culture_change }
			}
			send_interface_message = {
				type = msg_culture_conversion
				title = converted_culture_msg_title
				custom_tooltip = converted_from_old_culture_to_new_culture_tt
				right_icon = scope:converting_character
			}
		}
	}
}

create_h_narsokratia_effect = {
	root = {
		primary_title = {
			save_scope_as = old_title
		}
	}

	save_scope_as = new_hegemon

	scope:new_hegemon = {
		hidden_effect = {
			create_dynamic_title = {
				tier = hegemony
				name = nars_hegemony_dynamic_name
				adj = nars_hegemony_dynamic_adj
				article = article_CONFEDERATION_CULTURE_NAME
			}
			scope:new_title = {
				set_title_color = { 240 240 220 }
				set_definitive_form = no
				set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
				set_can_use_nomadic_naming = no
				if = {
					limit = { exists = scope:new_hegemon.house }
					set_coa = scope:new_hegemon.house
				}
				else = {
					generate_coa = yes
				}
			}
		}

		gain_heroic_legend_seed_tooltip_effect = yes

		dynasty ?= { add_dynasty_prestige = 5000 }
		
		hidden_effect = {
			# Fold every kingdom-tier title in the new hegemon's realm into the
			# hegemony's de jure structure -- whether held personally or held by a
			# vassal within the new hegemon's realm (holder.top_liege).
			every_kingdom = {
				limit = {
					is_titular = no
					NOT = { de_jure_liege = scope:new_title }
					OR = {
						holder = scope:new_hegemon
						holder.top_liege ?= scope:new_hegemon
					}
				}
				set_de_jure_liege_title = scope:new_title
			}
		}
		create_title_and_vassal_change = {
			type = created
			save_scope_as = change
			add_claim_on_loss = no
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			scope:new_title = {
				change_title_holder = {
					holder = root
					change = scope:change
				}
			}
		}
		resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:change
		
		hidden_effect = {
			set_primary_title_to = scope:new_title
		}
		
		hidden_effect = {
			spawn_army = {
				name = event_nars_troop_name
				men_at_arms = {
					type = nars_t2
					stacks = 15
				}
				men_at_arms = {
					type = nars_t1
					stacks = 85
				}
				location = this.capital_province
				uses_supply = yes
				inheritable = yes
			}
		}
		
		hidden_effect = {
			selian_vassal_conversion_effect = yes
			selian_major_county_effect = yes
		}
		
	}
}

nars_maa_setup_effect = {
	create_maa_regiment = {
			type = nars_t1
			check_can_recruit = no
			size = 3
	}
}

	}
}﻿set_politeion_effect = {
	# Remove any previous personal god relation

	if = {
		limit = { is_archaic = yes }
		if = {	
			limit = {
				has_character_modifier = politeion_selian_narses
			}
			add_piety = massive_piety_loss
			remove_character_modifier = politeion_selian_narses
		}
		else_if = {	
			limit = {
				has_character_modifier = politeion_selian_phokas
			}
			add_piety = massive_piety_loss
			remove_character_modifier = politeion_selian_phokas
		}
		else_if = {	
			limit = {
				has_character_modifier = politeion_selian_enuo
			}
			add_piety = massive_piety_loss
			remove_character_modifier = politeion_selian_enuo
		}
		else = {
			add_piety = massive_piety_loss
		}
	}
	add_character_modifier = $POLITEION$
}﻿intro_standard_effect = {
	add_character_modifier = {
		modifier = intro_modifier
	}

# Stress loss and a lingering modifier, scaled by the Luxuries option.
# scope = participant
nars_orgio_luxuries_rewards_effect = {
	# Clear a leftover modifier from an earlier Orgio so tiers never stack.
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = nars_orgio_revelry_normal_modifier }
			remove_character_modifier = nars_orgio_revelry_normal_modifier
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = nars_orgio_revelry_enhanced_modifier }
			remove_character_modifier = nars_orgio_revelry_enhanced_modifier
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = nars_orgio_revelry_lavish_modifier }
			remove_character_modifier = nars_orgio_revelry_lavish_modifier
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { nars_orgio_luxuries_lavish_trigger = yes }
		stress_impact = {
			base = major_stress_loss
			lustful = major_stress_loss
			sadistic = medium_stress_loss
		}
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = nars_orgio_revelry_lavish_modifier
			years = 2
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { nars_orgio_luxuries_enhanced_trigger = yes }
		stress_impact = {
			base = medium_stress_loss
			lustful = major_stress_loss
			sadistic = medium_stress_loss
		}
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = nars_orgio_revelry_enhanced_modifier
			years = 2
		}
	}
	else = {
		stress_impact = {
			base = minor_stress_loss
			lustful = major_stress_loss
			sadistic = medium_stress_loss
		}
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = nars_orgio_revelry_normal_modifier
			years = 2
		}
	}
}

# Every guest thinks better of the host, scaled by the Luxuries option.
# scope = activity
nars_orgio_guest_opinion_effect = {
	every_attending_character = {
		limit = { NOT = { this = scope:host } }
		custom = every_nars_orgio_participant
		if = {
			limit = { nars_orgio_luxuries_lavish_trigger = yes }
			add_opinion = {
				target = scope:host
				modifier = pleased_opinion
				opinion = 30
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { nars_orgio_luxuries_enhanced_trigger = yes }
			add_opinion = {
				target = scope:host
				modifier = pleased_opinion
				opinion = 20
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = scope:host
				modifier = pleased_opinion
				opinion = 10
			}
		}
	}
}

# Rolls for the host to overhear a guest's secret during the Orgio phase.
# scope = host
nars_orgio_host_secret_roll_effect = {
	random = {
		chance = nars_orgio_secret_chance_value
		trigger_event = {
			id = nars_orgio.3090
			days = { 9 12 }
		}
	}
}

# Conclusion rewards for the host.
disburse_nars_orgio_host_rewards_effect = {
	scope:activity = {
		add_activity_log_entry = {
			key = host_smalldeviant_orgy
			tags = { completed }
			# this line below adds the entry to the Effects section of the conclusion UI
			show_in_conclusion = yes
			character = scope:host
			
			scope:host = {
				nars_orgio_luxuries_rewards_effect = yes
			}
			nars_orgio_guest_opinion_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

# Conclusion rewards for every guest.
# scope = guest
disburse_nars_orgio_guest_rewards_effect = {
	nars_orgio_luxuries_rewards_effect = yes
}
﻿nars_naturalis_set_tier_xp_effect = {
    # 1. Wipe the track clean so values do not stack
    add_trait_xp = {
        trait = nars_naturalis
        track = polimetis
        value = -100
    }

    # 2. Apply the correct XP threshold based on Education Level
    if = {
        limit = { has_education_rank_5_trigger = yes } 
        add_trait_xp = {
            trait = nars_naturalis
            track = polimetis
            value = 100
        }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { has_education_rank_4_trigger = yes }
        add_trait_xp = {
            trait = nars_naturalis
            track = polimetis
            value = 80
        }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { has_education_rank_3_trigger = yes }
        add_trait_xp = {
            trait = nars_naturalis
            track = polimetis
            value = 60
        }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { has_education_rank_2_trigger = yes }
        add_trait_xp = {
            trait = nars_naturalis
            track = polimetis
            value = 40
        }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { has_education_rank_1_trigger = yes }
        add_trait_xp = {
            trait = nars_naturalis
            track = polimetis
            value = 20
        }
    }
}

nars_apply_setup_effect = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            OR = {
                is_ethnically_selian = yes
            }
            NOT = { has_trait = nars_naturalis }
        }
        add_trait = nars_naturalis
        nars_naturalis_set_tier_xp_effect = yes
    }
}
﻿recruit_nars_add_courtier_effect = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = host

	every_realm_province = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				save_temporary_scope_as = potential_province
				NOT = {
					any_in_list = {
						list = pool_provinces
						county.duchy = scope:potential_province.county.duchy
					}
				}
			}
			add_to_temporary_list = pool_provinces
		}
	}

	every_in_list = {
		list = pool_provinces
		save_temporary_scope_as = pool_province

		every_pool_character = {
			province = scope:pool_province
			limit = {
				culture = { has_cultural_pillar = heritage_nars }
				guest_knight_candidate_trigger = { HOST = root }
			}
			add_to_temporary_list = potential_nars_courtiers
		}
	}

	random_in_list = {
		list = potential_nars_courtiers
		limit = { is_alive = yes }
		weight = {
			base = 1
			modifier = {
				add = {
					value = prowess
					subtract = 15
					multiply = 5
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = {
					value = learning
					subtract = 15
					multiply = 5
				}
			}
		}
		save_scope_as = selian_courtier
	}

	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { exists = scope:selian_courtier } }
		create_character = {
			location = root.capital_province
			culture = culture:selian
			faith = root.faith
			rite = root.rite
			random_traits_list = {
				count = 2
				wrathful = {}
				arrogant = {}
				diligent = {}
				humble = {}
				calm = {}
				impatient = {}
				greedy = {}
				temperate = {}
				ambitious = {}
				just = {}
				stubborn = {}
			}
			random_traits_list = {
				count = 1
				lustful = {}
				arrogant = {}
				chaste = {}
			}
			random_traits_list = {
				count = 1
				education_martial_2 = {}
				education_martial_3 = {}
				education_martial_4 = {}
				education_learning_2 = {}
				education_learning_3 = {}
				education_learning_4 = {}
				education_stewardship_2 = {}
				education_stewardship_3 = {}
				education_stewardship_4 = {}
			}
			random_traits_list = {
				count = { 0 1 }
				lifestyle_blademaster = {}
				lifestyle_herbalist = {}
				scholar = {}
				lifestyle_poet = {}
			}
			learning = { 10 25 }
			intrigue = { 10 25 }
			stewardship = { 10 25 }
			diplomacy = { 10 25 }
			martial = { 10 25 }
			prowess = { 15 30 }
			gender_female_chance = 50
			save_scope_as = selian_courtier
			after_creation = {
				add_trait = nars_naturalis
				nars_naturalis_set_tier_xp_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}

	add_courtier = scope:selian_courtier

	send_interface_message = {
		title = nars_courtiers.0001.t
		desc = nars_courtiers.0001.desc
		type = event_prowess_good_desc
		left_icon = scope:selian_courtier
	}

	clear_saved_scope = selian_courtier

get_item_quality_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { exists = scope:quality }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = scope:random_quality_bonus } }
			random_list = {
				10 = { # Illustrious — rarest
					modifier = {
						add = 15
						scope:actor = {
							has_trait_track = { trait = lifestyle_pottery track = potter value >= 100 }
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						add = 8
						scope:actor = {
							has_trait_track = { trait = lifestyle_pottery track = potter value >= 75 }
						}
					}
					save_scope_value_as = { name = random_quality_bonus value = 200 }
				}
				20 = { # Famed
					modifier = {
						add = 15
						scope:actor = {
							has_trait_track = { trait = lifestyle_pottery track = potter value >= 75 }
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						add = 8
						scope:actor = {
							has_trait_track = { trait = lifestyle_pottery track = potter value >= 50 }
						}
					}
					save_scope_value_as = { name = random_quality_bonus value = 80 }
				}
				30 = { # Masterwork
					modifier = {
						add = 10
						scope:actor = {
							has_trait_track = { trait = lifestyle_pottery track = potter value >= 50 }
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						add = 5
						scope:actor = {
							has_trait_track = { trait = lifestyle_pottery track = potter value >= 25 }
						}
					}
					save_scope_value_as = { name = random_quality_bonus value = 40 }
				}
				40 = { # Common — most likely
					modifier = {
						add = -20
						scope:actor = {
							has_trait_track = { trait = lifestyle_pottery track = potter value >= 75 }
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						add = -10
						scope:actor = {
							has_trait_track = { trait = lifestyle_pottery track = potter value >= 25 }
						}
					}
					save_scope_value_as = { name = random_quality_bonus value = 0 }
				}
			}
		}
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = quality
			value = {
				value = 20
				add = scope:random_quality_bonus
				min = 1
				max = 200
			}
		}
	}
}

get_item_wealth_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { exists = scope:wealth }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = scope:random_wealth_bonus } }

							has_trait_track = { trait = lifestyle_pottery track = potter value >= 50 }
						}

						add = -20
						scope:actor = {
							has_trait_track = { trait = lifestyle_pottery track = potter value >= 75 }
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						add = -10

							has_trait_track = { trait = lifestyle_pottery track = potter value >= 25 }
						}
					}

				min = 1
				max = 200
			}
		}
	}

produce_amphora_effect = {
	$OWNER$ = { save_scope_as = owner }

	get_item_quality_effect = yes
	get_item_wealth_effect = yes

	hidden_effect_new_object = {
		create_artifact = {
			name = artifact_amphora_name
			description = artifact_placeholder_description
			type = amphora
			visuals = urn
			modifier = artifact_placeholder_modifier
			wealth = scope:wealth
			quality = scope:quality
			save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
			history = {
				type = created
				recipient = scope:owner
				location = scope:owner.location
			}
		}
		scope:newly_created_artifact = {
			get_artifact_feature_references_effect = yes
			set_artifact_description = artifact_amphora_description
			add_scaled_artifact_modifier_minor_prestige_effect = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						rarity = famed
						rarity = illustrious
					}
				}
				add_scaled_artifact_modifier_majesty_effect = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = { rarity = illustrious }
				add_scaled_artifact_modifier_dynasty_prestige_effect = yes
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = quality
				value = scope:quality
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = wealth
				value = scope:wealth
			}
		}
	}
}﻿recruit_nars_court_has_room_trigger = {
	OR = {
		is_ai = no	
		any_courtier = {
			count < 20
		}
	}
}

valid_for_selian_conversion_trigger = {
	# The character (the land's holder) must differ from root in BOTH faith and culture.
	NOR = {
		faith = root.faith
		follows_selian_faith = yes
		culture = root.culture
	}
	is_at_war = no
	NOR = {
		has_character_flag = do_not_disturb_preachers
		primary_title = { has_variable = do_not_disturb_preachers }
	}
}

nars_faith_needs_bribe_trigger = {
	nars_faith_conversion_resistance_value >= 15
}

nars_faith_scheme_has_targets_trigger = {
	any_sub_realm_title = {
		tier = tier_county
		NOR = {
			faith = root.faith
			follows_selian_faith = yes
		}
		holder = {
			valid_for_selian_conversion_trigger = yes
		}
	}
}

nars_is_ruler_scope_available = {
	county = {
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:ruler }
			holder = scope:ruler
		}
		trigger_else = {
			holder = $HOLDER$
		}
	}
}

is_barony_valid_for_aretaic_school = {
	nars_is_ruler_scope_available = {
		HOLDER = $HOLDER$
	}
	title_province = {
        has_special_building_slot = no
    }
	is_leased_out = no
	title_province = {
		NOT = { has_building_or_higher = aretaic_school_01 }
	}
}

		}
	}

	scope:activity = {

			category = orgio_option_luxuries
			option = orgio_luxuries_lavish
		}
	}
}

﻿nars_faith_conversion_resistance_value = {
	value = 0
	if = {
		limit = { has_trait = zealous }
		add = 20
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_trait = theologian }
		add = 15
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_trait = stubborn }
		add = 15
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_trait = arbitrary }
		add = 10
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_trait = arrogant }
		add = 10
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_trait = honest }
		add = 5
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_trait = cynical }
		subtract = 20
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_trait = greedy }
		subtract = 15
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_trait = craven }
		subtract = 15
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_trait = content }
		subtract = 10
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_trait = deceitful }
		subtract = 10
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_trait = trusting }
		subtract = 5
	}
}
﻿# Orgio Luxuries options (Normal / Enhanced / Lavish).

# Total cost of each option. The activity charges 4/5 of it in gold and 1/5 in piety.
# root = character planning the activity
nars_orgio_luxuries_normal_cost = {
	value = 100
	add = {
		value = 10
		multiply = activity_cost_scale_by_tier
		subtract = 10
	}
	add = {
		value = 10
		multiply = activity_cost_scale_by_era
		subtract = 10
	}
	min = 0
}

nars_orgio_luxuries_enhanced_cost = {
	value = 200
	add = {
		value = 20
		multiply = activity_cost_scale_by_tier
		subtract = 20
	}
	add = {
		value = 20
		multiply = activity_cost_scale_by_era
		subtract = 20
	}
	min = 0
}

nars_orgio_luxuries_lavish_cost = {
	value = 300
	add = {
		value = 30
		multiply = activity_cost_scale_by_tier
		subtract = 30
	}
	add = {
		value = 30
		multiply = activity_cost_scale_by_era
		subtract = 30
	}
	min = 0
}

# Chance (%) for the host to overhear a guest's secret.
nars_orgio_secret_chance_value = {
	value = 15
	if = {
		limit = { nars_orgio_luxuries_lavish_trigger = yes }
		value = 50
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { nars_orgio_luxuries_enhanced_trigger = yes }
		value = 30
	}
}

# Chance (%) for a participant to come away with the deviant trait.
nars_orgio_deviant_chance_value = {
	value = 5
	if = {
		limit = { nars_orgio_luxuries_lavish_trigger = yes }
		value = 10
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { nars_orgio_luxuries_enhanced_trigger = yes }
		value = 7
	}
}
﻿odds_nars_scheme_misc_value = {
	add = {
		value = {
			add = 15
			desc = BASE
		}
		
		add = {
			value = scope:owner.learning
			multiply = 0.9
			desc = scheme_odds.learning.owner
		}
		
		scope:target ?= {
			add = {
				value = scope:target.learning
				multiply = 0.9
				desc = scheme_odds.learning.target
			}
			add = {
				value = "opinion(scope:owner)"
				multiply = 0.5
				desc = scheme_odds.target.opinion_of_owner
			}
		}
	}
}﻿story_nars_hegemon = {
    visible = yes

    icon = {
        reference = "gfx/interface/icons/artifact/artifact_icons_constantine.dds"
    }

    background = {
        reference = "gfx/interface/illustrations/event_scenes/nars_war.dds"
    }

    visualization = {
        # character = {
        #     variable_name = "nars_char"
        #     label = "NARS_CHAR_LABEL"
        # }
        custom_string_key = "NIKATOR_STORY_PRACTICAL_INFORMATION_STRING"
    }

    on_setup = {
        story_owner = { 
            save_scope_as = nars_char
        }
        set_variable = {
            name = nars_char
            value = scope:nars_char
        }
    }
    
    # 1. Ongoing Events
    effect_group = { 
        days = { 180 420 }

        trigger = {
            story_owner = {
                is_available = yes
                is_alive = yes
                is_adult = yes
                top_liege = this
                has_trait = nars_hegemon
                NOT = { has_trait = conqueror }
            }
        }

        first_valid = {
            triggered_effect = {
                trigger = { always = yes }
                effect = {
                    story_owner = {
                        trigger_event = {
                            on_action = ongoing_nars_hegemon_events
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    # 2. AI Pacing Mechanics
    effect_group = {
        days = { 30 60 }

        triggered_effect = {
            trigger = {
                story_owner = { is_ai = yes }
            }
            effect = {
                story_owner = {
                    save_scope_as = root
                    ai_conqueror_yearly_effect = yes
                    if = { 
                        limit = {
                            OR = {
                                top_liege != this
                                is_landed = no
                                highest_held_title_tier <= tier_county
                                is_tributary = yes
                            }
                            is_ai = yes
                        }
                        # FIXED: Triggers custom event, not vanilla
                        trigger_event = nars_hegemon.0002
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    # 3. Cycle End Conditions (The 6 Triggers)
    effect_group = { 
        days = { 900 1800 }
    
        triggered_effect = {
            trigger = {
                story_owner = {
                    OR = {
                        AND = {
                            exists = var:lost_wars
                            var:lost_wars >= 3
                        }
                        is_ideal_selian = no
                        days_of_continuous_peace >= 3650 
                        is_independent_ruler = no
                        has_trait = incapable
                        highest_held_title_tier <= tier_duchy
                    }
                }
            }
            effect = {
                story_owner = {
                    trigger_event = nars_hegemon.0002
                }
                end_story = yes
            }
        }
    }

    on_owner_death = {
		story_owner = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					primary_heir ?= {
						is_physically_able_adult = yes
						OR = {
							ai_has_cautious_personality = no
							is_ai = no
						}
					}
				}
				primary_heir ?= {
					add_trait = nars_hegemon
					create_story = story_nars_hegemon
				}
			}
			else = {
				every_heir = {
					limit = {
						is_physically_able_adult = yes
						OR = {
							ai_has_cautious_personality = no
							is_ai = no
						}
					}
					add_trait = nars_hegemon
					create_story = story_nars_hegemon
				}
			}
		}
		end_story = yes
	}

    on_end = {
        debug_log = "Nikátōr story cycle ended on:"
        debug_log_date = yes
        # Removed event trigger here so it doesn't fire upon natural death
        story_owner = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_alive = yes
				}
				remove_trait = nars_hegemon
			}
		}
    }
}﻿story_produce_amphora = {
    visible = yes
    icon = { reference = "gfx/interface/icons/artifact/artifact_amphora.dds" }
    background = { reference = "gfx/interface/illustrations/event_scenes/nars_temple.dds" }

    visualization = {
        basic_counter = {
            variable_name = "produce_amphora_success"
            tooltip = "produce_amphora_COUNTER_TOOLTIP"
            min = 0
            max = 5
            label = "produce_amphora_VALUE_LABEL"
            min_label = "produce_amphora_VALUE_MIN_LABEL"
            max_label = "produce_amphora_VALUE_MAX_LABEL"
        }
    }

    on_setup = {
        set_variable = { name = produce_amphora_success value = 0 }
        root = {
            set_variable = {
                name = produce_amphora_story_ref
                value = prev
            }
        }
    }

    on_owner_death = { end_story = yes }
    on_end = {
        debug_log = "$decision_produce_amphora$ story cycle ended on:"
        debug_log_date = yes
    }

            label = "SPONSOR_MISSIONARIES_VALUE_LABEL"
            min_label = "SPONSOR_MISSIONARIES_VALUE_MIN_LABEL"
            max_label = "SPONSOR_MISSIONARIES_VALUE_MAX_LABEL"
        }
    }

    on_setup = {
        set_variable = { name = sponsor_meithios_success value = 0 }
    }

    # Safety net: end_scheme (called directly by the vanilla "Abandon Scheme" action,
    # special_interaction = remove_scheme_interaction in 00_scheme_interactions.txt) does
    # NOT run the scheme's own on_invalidated block, so a manually-abandoned scheme can
    # leave this story running forever. Check periodically and self-end if the owner no
    # longer has a sponsor_meithios_scheme for any reason.
    #
    # NOTE: this previously tracked the owner via a hand-rolled
    # var:sponsor_meithios_story_ref (set in on_setup to `prev`), but inside on_setup
    # `root` IS the story itself, so `root = { value = prev }` only ever captured the
    # story, never the owning character. any_scheme run on that wrong scope always
    # failed, so this safety net fired and ended the story on its very first 5-day
    # tick -- long before the scheme's first phase could ever complete. story_owner is
    # the built-in scope link CK3 already provides for a story's owning character (see
    # vanilla book_translation_story_cycle.txt's on_setup), so use that directly.
    effect_group = {
        days = 5
        trigger = {
            exists = story_owner
        }
        triggered_effect = {
            trigger = {
                NOT = {
                    story_owner = {
                        any_scheme = {
                            type = sponsor_meithios_scheme
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            effect = {
                end_story = yes
            }
        }
    }

    on_owner_death = { end_story = yes }
    on_end = {
        debug_log = "$decision_sponsor_meithios$ story cycle ended on:"
        debug_log_date = yes
    }
}

﻿﻿namespace = nars_construction

nars_construction.0001 = {
    type = character_event

    hidden = yes

    immediate = {
        play_music_cue = mx_cue_positive_effect
        random_sub_realm_county = {
            limit = {
                has_county_modifier = nars_construction_modifier
            }
            save_scope_as = construction_site
        }
        hidden_effect = {
            scope:construction_site = {
                remove_county_modifier = nars_construction_modifier
                title_province = {
                    remove_building = constructing_cranes_01
                    add_special_building = aretaic_school_01
                }
            }
        }
        send_interface_message = {
            type = event_prowess_good_desc
            title = nars_construction.0001.title
            desc = nars_construction.0001.desc
            left_icon = scope:construction_site.holder
        }
    }
}

#Standard Selian
nars_courtiers.0001 = {
	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		exists = var:number_of_invite_selian_countdown
	}

	immediate = {
		save_temporary_scope_as = host

		# Build a list of one province per duchy in the realm
		every_realm_province = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = potential_province
					NOT = {
						any_in_list = {
							list = pool_provinces
							county.duchy = scope:potential_province.county.duchy
						}
					}
				}
				add_to_temporary_list = pool_provinces
			}
		}

		# Gather Selian pool characters with suitable prowess
		every_in_list = {
			list = pool_provinces
			save_temporary_scope_as = pool_province

			every_pool_character = {
				province = scope:pool_province
				limit = {
					culture = { has_cultural_pillar = heritage_nars }
					guest_knight_candidate_trigger = { HOST = root }
				}
				add_to_temporary_list = potential_nars_courtiers
			}
		}

		# Pick the best candidate (weighted by how far above minimum prowess they are)
		random_in_list = {
			list = potential_nars_courtiers
			limit = { is_alive = yes }
			weight = {
				base = 1
				modifier = {
					add = {
						value = prowess
						subtract = 15
						multiply = 5
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = {
						value = learning
						subtract = 15
						multiply = 5
					}
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = selian_courtier
		}

		# If no suitable pool character exists, create a fresh Selian warrior
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = scope:selian_courtier } }
			create_character = {
				location = root.capital_province
				culture = culture:selian
				faith = root.faith
				rite = root.rite
				random_traits_list = {
                    count = 2
                    diligent = {}
                    impatient = {}
                    greedy = {}
                    temperate = {}
                    ambitious = {}
                    just = {}
                    stubborn = {}
                }
                random_traits_list = {
                    count = 1
                    wrathful = {}
                    calm = {}
                    lustful = {}
                    arrogant = {}
                    chaste = {}
                    humble = {}
                }
				random_traits_list = {
					count = 1
					education_martial_2 = {}
					education_martial_3 = {}
					education_martial_4 = {}
					education_learning_2 = {}
					education_learning_3 = {}
					education_learning_4 = {}
					education_stewardship_2 = {}
					education_stewardship_3 = {}
					education_stewardship_4 = {}
				}
				random_traits_list = {
					count = { 0 1 }
					lifestyle_blademaster = {}
					lifestyle_herbalist = {}
					scholar = {}
					lifestyle_poet = {}
				}
				learning = { 10 25 }
				intrigue = { 10 25 }
				stewardship = { 10 25 }
				diplomacy = { 10 25 }
				martial = { 10 25 }
				prowess = { 15 30 }
				gender_female_chance = 50
				save_scope_as = selian_courtier
				after_creation = {
					add_trait = nars_naturalis
					nars_naturalis_set_tier_xp_effect = yes
				}
			}
		}

		# Bring the courtier to court and notify the player
		add_courtier = scope:selian_courtier
		
		send_interface_message = {
			title = nars_courtiers.0001.t
			desc = nars_courtiers.0001.desc
			type = event_prowess_good_desc
			left_icon = scope:selian_courtier
		}

		# Count down and either end the chain or schedule the next arrival
		change_variable = {
			name = number_of_invite_selian_countdown
			subtract = 1
		}

		if = {
			limit = { var:number_of_invite_selian_countdown = 0 }
			remove_variable = number_of_invite_selian_countdown
		}
		else = {
			clear_saved_scope = selian_courtier
			trigger_event = {
				id = nars_courtiers.0001
				days = { 100 200 }
			}
		}
	}
}

                short_term_gold < medium_gold_value
                factor = 0
            }
        }
    }

    option = {
        name = nars_courtiers.0011.b
        flavor = nars_courtiers.0011.b.flavor
        trigger = {
            exists = scope:savant
        }
        scope:savant = {
            set_employer = root
        }
        pay_treasury_or_gold = {
            target = scope:savant
            value = massive_treasury_or_gold_value
        }
        ai_chance = {
            factor = 100
            modifier = {
                short_term_gold < medium_gold_value
                factor = 0
            }
        }
    }
	
    option = {
        name = nars_courtiers.0011.d
        flavor = nars_courtiers.0011.d.flavor
        ai_chance = { factor = 2 }
    }

	after = { 
		hidden_effect = {
			every_courtier = {
				nars_naturalis_set_tier_xp_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}
}﻿namespace = nars_faith

nars_faith.9000 = {
	hidden = yes
	type = character_event

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= empire }
			selian_major_county_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { highest_held_title_tier = kingdom }
			selian_moderate_county_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			selian_minor_county_effect = yes
		}

		send_interface_message = {
            title = nars_faith_9000_toast_title
        	desc = nars_faith_9000_toast_desc
			type = event_prowess_good_desc
        }
	}

# Sponsor Missionaries
nars_faith.0001 = {
    type = character_event
    title = nars_faith.0001.t
    desc = nars_faith.0001.desc
    theme = nars_faith
    
    left_portrait = {
        character = root
        animation = prayer
    }
    right_portrait = {
        character = scope:evangelizer
        animation = wedding_priest
    }

    lower_right_portrait = scope:pagan

    trigger = {
        nars_faith_scheme_has_targets_trigger = yes
    }

    immediate = {
        random_sub_realm_title = {
            limit = {
                tier = tier_county
                NOR = {
                    faith = root.faith
                    follows_selian_faith = yes
                }
                holder = {
                    valid_for_selian_conversion_trigger = yes
                }
            }
            save_scope_as = title_to_convert
            holder = {
                save_scope_as = pagan
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = {
                scope:pagan = { nars_faith_needs_bribe_trigger = yes }
            }
            save_scope_value_as = { name = needs_bribe value = yes }
        }
        else = {
            save_scope_value_as = { name = needs_bribe value = no }
        }
		hidden_effect = {
			random_pool_character = {
				province = root.location
				limit = {
					faith = root.faith
					learning >= decent_skill_rating
				}
				save_scope_as = evangelizer
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { exists = scope:evangelizer }
				}
				create_character = {
					location = root.location
					culture = culture:selian
					faith = root.faith
					rite = root.rite
					template = selian_template.1011
					gender_female_chance = 100
					save_scope_as = evangelizer
					after_creation = {
						add_trait = nars_naturalis
					}
				}
			}
			add_courtier = scope:evangelizer
			save_scope_as = sender
			location = { save_scope_as = starting_point }
		}
    }

    option = {
        name = nars_faith.0001.a
		flavor = nars_faith.0001.a.flavor
        custom_tooltip = nars_faith.0001.a.tt
        pay_short_term_gold = {
            target = scope:evangelizer
            gold = major_gold_value
        }
        add_piety = minor_piety_gain
        if = {
            limit = {
                any_vassal = { has_vassal_stance = zealot }
            }
            every_vassal = {
                limit = { has_vassal_stance = zealot }
                custom = every_zealot_vassal
                add_opinion = {
                    modifier = approved_opinion
                    target = root
                    opinion = 5
                }
            }
        }
        scope:evangelizer = {
            start_travel_plan = {
                destination = scope:pagan.capital_province
                on_arrival_event = nars_faith.0002
                on_arrival_destinations = all
                return_trip = no # One way
            }
        }
    }
}

nars_faith.0002 = {
    hidden = yes

    immediate = {
        scope:pagan = { add_visiting_courtier = scope:evangelizer }
        if = {
            limit = {
                scope:pagan = {
                    OR = {
                        is_alive = no
                        is_landed = no
                    }
                }
            }
            scope:title_to_convert.holder = { save_scope_as = pagan }
        }

        if = {
            limit = {
                NOT = {
                    scope:title_to_convert.holder = {
                        valid_for_selian_conversion_trigger = yes
                    }
                }
            }
            scope:evangelizer = {
                start_travel_plan = {
                    destination = scope:starting_point
                    return_trip = no
                }
            }
        }
        else = {
            scope:title_to_convert.faith = { save_scope_as = old_faith }
            # Player event for scope:pagan
            if = {
                limit = {
                    scope:pagan = { is_ai = no }
                }
                scope:pagan = {
                    trigger_event = {
                        id = nars_faith.0003
                        days = 1
                    }
                }
            }
            # AI event for the evangelizer
            else = {
                scope:evangelizer = {
                    trigger_event = {
                        id = nars_faith.0004
                        days = 1
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

nars_faith.0003 = {
    type = character_event
    title = nars_faith.0001.t
    desc = {
        first_valid = {
            triggered_desc = {
                trigger = { scope:needs_bribe = yes }
                desc = nars_faith.0003.desc_bribe
            }
            desc = nars_faith.0003.desc_no_bribe
        }
    }

    theme = nars_faith
    
    trigger = {
        scope:evangelizer = { is_alive = yes }
    }

    left_portrait = {
        character = root
        animation = thinking
    }
    right_portrait = {
        character = scope:evangelizer
        animation = wedding_priest
    }
    lower_right_portrait = scope:sender

    option = {
        name = nars_faith.0003.a
        flavor = nars_faith.0003.a.flavor

        scope:evangelizer = {
            add_character_flag = success_conversion
        }
        if = {
            limit = { scope:needs_bribe = yes }
            scope:evangelizer = {
                pay_short_term_gold = {
                    target = root
                    gold = major_gold_value
                }
            }
        }
        else = {
            scope:evangelizer = {
                pay_short_term_gold = {
                    target = root
                    gold = major_gold_value
                }
            }
        }
        
        scope:title_to_convert = {
            set_county_faith = scope:evangelizer.faith
        }

        # Stress impact adjusted
        stress_impact = {
            zealous = medium_stress_impact_gain
            content = medium_stress_impact_gain
            arrogant = minor_stress_impact_gain
        }
    }

    option = {
        name = nars_faith.0003.b
        flavor = nars_faith.0003.b.flavor

        scope:title_to_convert = {
            set_county_faith = scope:evangelizer.faith
        }

        scope:evangelizer = {
            add_character_flag = success_conversion
        }

        if = {
            limit = { exists = liege }
            liege = {
                add_opinion = {
                    target = root
                    modifier = approval_opinion
                    opinion = 15
                    years = 3
                }
            } 
        }
        
        stress_impact = {
            zealous = medium_stress_impact_gain
            content = medium_stress_impact_gain
            arrogant = minor_stress_impact_gain
            greedy = medium_stress_impact_gain
        }
    }

    option = {
        name = nars_faith.0003.c
        flavor = nars_faith.0003.c.flavor
        add_piety = major_piety_gain

        scope:evangelizer = {
            add_character_flag = failed_conversion
        }

        scope:title_to_convert = {
            set_variable = {
                name = do_not_disturb_preachers
                value = yes
            }
        }

        if = {
            limit = { exists = liege }
            liege = {
                add_opinion = {
                    target = root
                    modifier = refused_bribe_opinion
                    opinion = -30
                    years = 3
                }
            } 
        }
    }

    after = {
        if = {
            limit = {
                scope:sender = { is_alive = yes }
                scope:evangelizer = { is_alive = yes }
            }
            scope:evangelizer = {
                start_travel_plan = {
                    destination = scope:sender.location
                    on_arrival_event = nars_faith.0005
                    on_arrival_destinations = all
                    return_trip = no # One way
                }
            }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                scope:evangelizer = { is_alive = yes }
            }
            scope:evangelizer = {
                start_travel_plan = {
                    destination = scope:starting_point
                    on_arrival_event = nars_faith.0005
                    on_arrival_destinations = all
                    return_trip = no # One way
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

nars_faith.0004 = {
    type = character_event
    hidden = yes
    
    trigger = {
        scope:evangelizer = { is_alive = yes }
    }

    immediate = {
        scope:evangelizer = {
            duel = {
                skill = learning
                target = scope:pagan
                50 = { # Convert
                    compare_modifier = {
                        value = scope:duel_value
                        multiplier = 2.0
                        min = -49
                    }
                    modifier = {
                        scope:pagan = {
                            has_trait = fickle
                        }
                        add = 10
                    }
                    modifier = {
                        scope:pagan = {
                            has_trait = craven
                        }
                        add = 10
                    }
                    modifier = {
                        scope:pagan = {
                            has_trait = intellect_bad
                        }
                        add = 10
                    }
                    modifier = {
                        scope:pagan = { primary_title = { tier = tier_empire } }
                        add = -25
                        }
                    modifier = {
                        scope:pagan = { primary_title = { tier = tier_kingdom } }
                        add = -10
                    }
                    scope:title_to_convert = {
                        set_county_faith = scope:evangelizer.faith
                    }
                    create_character_memory = {
                        type = was_evangelized_memory
                        participants = { missionary = scope:evangelizer sender = scope:sender }
                    }
                    scope:sender = {
                        create_character_memory = {
                            type = evangelized_memory
                            participants = { missionary = scope:evangelizer pagan = scope:pagan }
                        }
                    }
                    if = {
                        limit = { scope:needs_bribe = yes }
                        scope:sender = { add_gold = medium_gold_value_negative }
                        scope:pagan = { add_gold = medium_gold_value }
                    }
                    else = {
                        scope:sender = { add_gold = minor_gold_value_negative }
                        scope:pagan = { add_gold = minor_gold_value }
                    }
                    scope:evangelizer = {
                        add_character_flag = success_conversion
                    }
                }
                35 = { # Not convert
                    compare_modifier = {
                        value = scope:duel_value
                        multiplier = -3.5
                        min = -49
                    }
                    modifier = {
                        scope:pagan = {
                            has_trait = theologian
                        }
                        add = 10
                    }
                    modifier = {
                        scope:pagan = {
                            has_trait = stubborn
                        }
                        add = 10
                    }
                    modifier = {
                        scope:pagan = { primary_title = { tier = tier_empire } }
                        add = 20
                    }
                    modifier = {
                        scope:pagan = { primary_title = { tier = tier_kingdom } }
                        add = 8
                    }
                    scope:pagan = { add_piety = medium_piety_gain }
                    scope:evangelizer = {
                        add_character_flag = failed_conversion
                    }
                }
                15 = { # They _really_ don't wanna convert
                    compare_modifier = {
                        value = scope:duel_value
                        multiplier = -3.5
                        min = -49
                    }
                    modifier = {
                        scope:pagan = {
                            has_trait = sadistic
                        }
                        add = 5
                    }
                    modifier = {
                        scope:pagan = {
                            has_trait = callous
                        }
                        add = 5
                    }
                    modifier = {
                        scope:pagan = {
                            has_trait = torturer
                        }
                        add = 5
                    }
                    modifier = {
                        scope:pagan = { primary_title = { tier = tier_empire } }
                        add = 15
                    }
                    modifier = {
                        scope:pagan = { primary_title = { tier = tier_kingdom } }
                        add = 5
                    }
                    scope:pagan = { add_piety = major_piety_gain }
                    scope:evangelizer = {
                        death = {
                            death_reason = death_execution
                            killer = root
                        }
                    }
                    scope:sender = {
                        trigger_event = {
                            id = nars_faith.0007
                            days = 3
                        }
                    }
                    scope:pagan = {
                        add_character_flag = do_not_disturb_preachers
                    }
                    scope:title_to_convert = {
                        set_variable = {
                            name = do_not_disturb_preachers
                            value = yes
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    after = {
        if = {
            limit = {
                scope:sender = { is_alive = yes }
                scope:evangelizer = { is_alive = yes }
            }
            scope:evangelizer = {
                start_travel_plan = {
                    destination = scope:sender.location
                    on_arrival_event = nars_faith.0005
                    on_arrival_destinations = all
                    return_trip = no # One way
                }
            }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                scope:evangelizer = { is_alive = yes }
            }
            scope:evangelizer = {
                start_travel_plan = {
                    destination = scope:starting_point
                    on_arrival_event = nars_faith.0005
                    on_arrival_destinations = all
                    return_trip = no # One way
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

                ai_vengefulness = 1
            }

	}

# Day 0: Setup
nars_hegemony.0001 = {
	type = character_event
	hidden = yes

	immediate = {
		add_character_flag = nikator_aspirant

		every_vassal_or_below = {
			limit = {
				is_ai = no
			}
            trigger_event = {
                id = nars_hegemony.0002
                days = 3
            }
        }

		every_in_list = {
			list = selian_coalition_members
			limit = {
				is_ai = no
			}
            trigger_event = {
                id = nars_hegemony.0003
                days = 3
            }
		}

		trigger_event = {
            id = nars_hegemony.0004
            days = 7
        }
	}
}

        }

# Day 7: War Declaration
nars_hegemony.0004 = {
	hidden = yes
	type = character_event

	immediate = {
		every_neighboring_and_across_water_top_liege_realm_owner = {
				limit = {
					is_independent_ruler = yes
					NOT = { has_alliance = root }
					NOT = { has_truce = root }
				}
				add_to_variable_list = selian_coalition_members
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_in_list = { list = selian_coalition_members }
			}
			
			ordered_in_list = {
				list = selian_coalition_members
				order_by = current_military_strength
				save_scope_as = coalition_war_leader
			}

			scope:coalition_war_leader = {
				start_war = {
					target = root
					target_title = root.primary_title
					cb = selian_defiance_cb
				}
			}
			root = {
				random_character_war = {
					limit = { primary_attacker = scope:coalition_war_leader }
					every_in_list = {
						list = selian_coalition_members
						limit = {
							NOT = { this = scope:coalition_war_leader }
						}
						prev = {
							add_attacker = prev
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
