nars_hegemon.0001 = {
    type = character_event
    title = nars_hegemon.0001.t
    desc = nars_hegemon.0001.desc
    window = big_event_window
    theme = emperor
    override_background = wilderness_mountains
    widget = { gui = event_window_widget_vfx_godray container = foreground_shader_vfx_container }

    left_portrait = {
        character = root
        animation = war_over_win
        camera = camera_ruler_designer_head_profile_90
    }

    immediate = {
    }

    option = { 
        name = nars_hegemon.0001.a
        flavor = narsokratia_event.flavor

		add_legitimacy = monumental_legitimacy_gain
        give_nickname = nick_soter
        add_trait = nars_hegemon
		
		create_story = story_nars_hegemon

		custom_tooltip = { 
			text = nars_dynasty_get_scion_tt
			dynasty = {
				every_dynasty_member = {
					limit = {
						is_alive = yes
						is_selian = yes
						NOT = { has_trait = nars_scion }
						OR = {
							is_close_family_of = root
							is_extended_family_of = root
						}
					}
					add_trait = nars_scion
				}
			}
		}

        legend_seed_great_deed_dynasty_effect = yes

		if = {
            limit = { has_dlc_feature = legends }
            create_legend_seed = {
                type = heroic
                quality = famed
                chronicle = conqueror_chronicle
            }
        }
    }
}

nars_hegemon.0002 = {
    type = character_event
    title = nars_hegemon.0002.t

    desc = {
        desc = nars_hegemon.0002.intro
        random_valid = {
            triggered_desc = {
                trigger = { days_of_continuous_peace >= 3650 }
                desc = nars_hegemon.0002.peace
            }
            triggered_desc = {
                trigger = {
                    AND = {
                        exists = var:lost_wars
                        var:lost_wars >= 3
                    }
                }
                desc = nars_hegemon.0002.lost_wars
            }
            triggered_desc = {
                trigger = { is_ideal_selian = no }
                desc = nars_hegemon.0002.traitor
            }
            triggered_desc = {
                trigger = { is_independent_ruler = no }
                desc = nars_hegemon.0002.subjugated
            }
            triggered_desc = {
                trigger = { has_trait = incapable }
                desc = nars_hegemon.0002.incapable
            }
            triggered_desc = {
                trigger = { highest_held_title_tier <= tier_duchy }
                desc = nars_hegemon.0002.demoted
            }
        }
        desc = nars_hegemon.0002.outro
    }
    
    theme = emperor
    window = big_event_window
    override_background = throne_room_east
    left_portrait = {
        character = root
        animation = sword_yield_start
    }
    immediate = {
		save_scope_as = root_scope
		every_owned_story = {
			type = story_nars_hegemon
			end_story = yes
		}
	}
    option = {
        name = nars_hegemon.0002.a
        flavor = nars_hegemon.0002.a.flavor
        remove_trait = nars_hegemon
        add_prestige_level = -2
        
        if = {
            limit = {
                any_vassal = { has_vassal_stance = glory_hound }
            }
            every_vassal = {
                limit = { has_vassal_stance = glory_hound }
                custom = every_glory_hound_vassal
                add_opinion = {
                    modifier = disappointed_opinion
                    target = root
                    opinion = -50
                }
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = {
                any_vassal = { has_vassal_stance = belligerent }
            }
            every_vassal = {
                limit = { has_vassal_stance = belligerent }
                custom = every_belligerent_vassal
                add_opinion = {
                    modifier = disappointed_opinion
                    target = root
                    opinion = -50
                }
            }
        }
        ai_chance = { base = 100 }
    }
}

# Small gift from County
nars_hegemon.0100 = {
	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		any_sub_realm_county = {
			exists = holder
			county_control >= 100
			county_opinion >= 0
			title_province = {
				available_loot >= 5
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_sub_realm_county = {
			limit = {
				exists = holder
				county_control >= 100
				county_opinion >= 0
				title_province = {
					available_loot >= 5
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = county_target
		}
		else = {
			send_interface_message = {
				type = event_diplomacy_good_with_text
				title = nars_hegemon_feed_county_gift
				desc = nars_hegemon_feed_county_gift_desc
				left_icon = scope:county_target
				add_gold = scope:county_target.title_province.available_loot
			}
		}
	}
}

# Good Knight Joins
nars_hegemon.0200 = {
	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			number_of_knights < max_number_of_knights
			any_knight = {
				prowess < invite_knights_decision_standard_value
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		save_temporary_scope_as = host

		#Set up a list of provinces, one per duchy in the realm, find all relevant pool characters there
		every_realm_province = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = potential_province
					NOT = {
						any_in_list = {
							list = pool_provinces
							county.duchy = scope:potential_province.county.duchy
						}
					}
				}
				add_to_temporary_list = pool_provinces
			}
		}

		every_in_list = {
			list = pool_provinces
			save_temporary_scope_as = pool_province

			every_pool_character = {
				province = scope:pool_province
				limit = {
					prowess >= invite_knights_decision_standard_value
					is_ideal_selian = yes
					guest_knight_candidate_trigger = { HOST = root }
				}
				add_to_temporary_list = potential_knights
			}
		}

		#Randomize a good knight from the realm pool characters
		random_in_list = {
			list = potential_knights
			limit = { is_alive = yes }
			weight = {
				base = 1
				modifier = {
					add = {
						value = prowess
						subtract = invite_knights_decision_standard_value
						multiply = 5
					}
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = knight
		}

		#If no one was found, make one
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = scope:knight } }

			create_character = {
				gender_female_chance = 50
				location = root.capital_province
				template = selian_template.1002
				culture = culture:selian
				faith = root.faith
				rite = root.rite
				add_trait = nars_naturalis
				save_scope_as = knight
				prowess = { 15 45 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:knight = {
					NOT = { has_trait = tourney_participant }
					is_lowborn = no
				}
			}
			random_list = {
				40 = {}
				60 = {
					trigger = { has_dlc_feature = tours_and_tournaments }
					scope:knight = {
						add_trait = tourney_participant
						add_random_tourney_participant_level_effect = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		send_interface_message = {
			type = event_diplomacy_good_with_text
			title = nars_hegemon_feed_knight_joins
			desc = nars_hegemon_feed_knight_joins_desc
			left_icon = scope:knight
			add_courtier = scope:knight
			add_hook = {
			type = loyalty_hook
				target = scope:knight
			}
		}
	}
}

	}

					has_vassal_stance = parochial
					has_any_bad_relationship_with_character_trigger = { CHARACTER = root }
				}

					target = root
					value <= -30
				}
			}

		}
		
		send_interface_message = {

# Courtier Gives you Gold
nars_hegemon.0400 = {
	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		any_courtier = {
			is_available_ai_adult = yes
			NOT = {
				has_any_bad_relationship_with_character_trigger = { CHARACTER = root }
			}
			gold >= 5
			ai_greed <= 20
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_available_ai_adult = yes
				NOT = {
					has_any_bad_relationship_with_character_trigger = { CHARACTER = root }
				}
				gold >= 5
				ai_greed <= 20
			}
			save_scope_as = generous_courtier
		}
		
		send_interface_message = {
			type = event_diplomacy_good_with_text
			title = nars_hegemon_feed_generous_courtier
			desc = nars_hegemon_feed_generous_courtier_desc
			left_icon = scope:generous_courtier
			scope:generous_courtier = {
				pay_short_term_gold = {
					target = root
					gold = {
						value = root.medium_gold_value
						max = scope:generous_courtier.gold
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

			}

	}

		recruit_terrain_specialist_effect = {
			SPECIALIST_TRAIT = military_engineer

		}
		scope:new_commander = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_trait = military_engineer
					}
				}
				add_trait = military_engineer
			}
		}
		send_interface_message = {
			type = event_diplomacy_good_with_text
			title = nars_hegemon_feed_siege_engineer
			desc = nars_hegemon_feed_siege_engineer_desc
			left_icon = scope:new_commander
			show_as_tooltip = {
				add_courtier = scope:new_commander
			}
		}
	}
}

# Proper Events
scripted_trigger happy_vassal_trigger = {
	is_available_ai_adult = yes
	is_landed = yes
	NOR = {
		has_any_bad_relationship_with_character_trigger = { CHARACTER = root }
		has_vassal_stance = parochial
		is_a_faction_member = yes
		is_at_war = yes
	}
	opinion = {
		target = root
		value >= -10
	}
}

scripted_trigger generous_vassal_trigger = {
	happy_vassal_trigger = yes
	gold >= root.medium_gold_value
	ai_greed <= 0
}

        character = scope:optimatos
        camera = camera_event_right

            trait = gallivanter
            trait = viking
            trait = adventurer
            save_scope_as = optimatos
        }
		scope:optimatos = { add_character_flag = wear_armor }
    }

    # Option A: Pay gold for special soldiers
    option = {
        name = nars_hegemon.1100.a # "I will pay you handsomely for your spears."

            set_employer = root

            location = root.capital_province
			uses_supply = yes
			inheritable = yes
		}
        remove_treasury_or_gold = { 300 800 }

			generous = medium_stress_impact_loss
			zealous = medium_stress_impact_loss
			brave = medium_stress_impact_loss
			ambitious = medium_stress_impact_loss
			content = medium_stress_impact_gain
			cynical = medium_stress_impact_gain
			greedy = medium_stress_impact_gain
		}
    }
    option = {
        name = nars_hegemon.1100.b
		flavor = nars_hegemon.1100.b.flavor
        
        random_list = {
            50 = {
                modifier = {
                    add = 10
                    diplomacy >= 25
                }
                modifier = {
                    add = 20
                    diplomacy >= 35

					name = event_troop_default_name

nars_hegemon.1300 = {
    type = character_event
    title = nars_hegemon.1300.t
    desc = nars_hegemon.1300.desc
    theme = emperor
	override_background = throne_room_east

    trigger = {
        has_trait = nars_hegemon
        any_sub_realm_county = {
            NOT = { culture = culture:selian }
        }
    }

    left_portrait = {
        character = root
        camera = camera_event_left
        animation = thinking
    }
    
    right_portrait = {
        character = scope:commander_1
        camera = camera_event_right
        animation = throne_room_one_handed_passive_1
    }

        animation = anger
    }

    lower_center_portrait = {

        animation = disapproval
    }

	lower_right_portrait = {
		character = scope:commander_4

		}

            age = { 30 55 }

		}

        create_character = {
            age = { 30 55 }
            martial = { 16 26 }
            trait = education_martial_3
			template = selian_template.1002
            gender = male
            culture = culture:selian
            faith = root.faith
            rite = root.rite
            save_scope_as = commander_4
			prowess = { 15 30 }
        }
        scope:commander_4 = { 
			add_to_list = vanguard_commanders_list 
			add_character_flag = wear_armor
		}
    }

    option = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = nars_vanguard_spoils_modifier }
		}
        name = nars_hegemon.1300.a
        remove_short_term_gold = major_gold_value
        add_character_modifier = {
            modifier = nars_vanguard_spoils_modifier
            years = 3 
        }
		hidden_effect = {
			every_in_list = {
				list = vanguard_commanders_list

nars_hegemon.1400 = {
    type = character_event
    title = nars_hegemon.1400.t
    desc = nars_hegemon.1400.desc
    theme = emperor

    trigger = {
        has_trait = nars_hegemon
        # Must have at least one foreign vassal who rules over a county of their own culture
        any_vassal = {
            NOT = { culture = culture:selian }
            any_held_county = { # FIXED: Valid CK3 syntax for domain
                culture = prev.culture
            }
        }
    }

    override_background = throne_room_east

    lower_left_portrait = {
        character = root
    }
    left_portrait = {
        character = scope:foreign_vassal_1
        animation = map_dismissal
		camera = camera_event_left_wedding
    }
    right_portrait = {
        character = scope:foreign_vassal_2
        camera = camera_event_right_away
        animation = scheme
    }

    immediate = {
        # 1. Grab the primary foreign vassal and their culture/county
        random_vassal = {
            limit = { 
                NOT = { culture = culture:selian }
                any_held_county = { # FIXED
                    culture = prev.culture 
                }
            }
            save_scope_as = foreign_vassal_1
            culture = { save_scope_as = foreign_culture }
            
            # Scope directly into their domain to guarantee a valid county
            random_held_county = { # FIXED
                limit = { culture = scope:foreign_culture }
                save_scope_as = unrest_county
            }
        }
        
        # 2. Grab up to 2 other foreign vassals (if they exist) to flesh out the event
        random_vassal = {
            limit = { 
                NOT = { culture = culture:selian }
                NOT = { this = scope:foreign_vassal_1 }
            }
            save_scope_as = foreign_vassal_2
        }
    }

    # Option A: Mysterious deaths (County conversion up, control down)
    option = {
        name = nars_hegemon.1400.a
        add_dread = 30
        scope:unrest_county = {
            change_county_control = -15
            add_county_modifier = {
                modifier = nars_brutal_purges_modifier
                days = 3650 # 10 years
            }
			set_county_culture = culture:selian
			set_county_faith = root.faith
			set_county_rite = root.rite
        }
        
        # Mysterious deaths 
        scope:foreign_vassal_1 = { death = { death_reason = death_court_intrigue } } 
        if = {
            limit = { exists = scope:foreign_vassal_2 }
            scope:foreign_vassal_2 = { death = { death_reason = death_killed_by_harem_slave} } 
        }
    }

    # Option B: Integration (Pay gold/prestige, gain acceptance/dev)
    option = {
        name = nars_hegemon.1400.b
        remove_short_term_gold = 75
        add_prestige = -150
        
        scope:foreign_culture = {
            change_cultural_acceptance = { # FIXED: Valid CK3 syntax
                target = root.culture
                value = 15
            }
        }
        scope:unrest_county = {
            change_county_development = 1 # FIXED: Valid CK3 syntax
			add_county_modifier = {
				modifier = county_increased_opinion_modifier
				years = 25
			}
        }
        scope:foreign_vassal_1 = {
            add_opinion = {
                target = root
                modifier = respect_opinion
				opinion = 15
            }
        }
		scope:foreign_vassal_2 = {
            add_opinion = {
                target = root
                modifier = respect_opinion
				opinion = 15
            }
        }
    }

    # Option C: Do nothing
    option = {
        name = nars_hegemon.1400.c
        add_stress = minor_stress_gain
    }
}﻿namespace = nars_maintenance

nars_orgio.3001 = {
	type = letter_event
	opening = nars_orgio.3001.t
	desc = nars_orgio.3001.desc
	sender = scope:host
	orphan = yes # deprecated by new activity system

	immediate = {
		scope:activity.activity_host = { save_scope_as = host }
		debug_log = invitation_received
	}

  	#Yes!
	option = {
		name = nars_orgio.3001.a

		play_music_cue = "mx_cue_sacredrite"
		accept_activity_invite = scope:activity
		reverse_add_opinion = {
			target = scope:host
			modifier = pleased_opinion
			opinion = 10
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			opinion_modifier = {
				opinion_target = scope:host
			}
		}
	}

	#No...
	option = {
		name = nars_orgio.3001.b

		decline_activity_invite = scope:activity
		reverse_add_opinion = {
			target = scope:host
			modifier = disappointed_opinion
			opinion = -10
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = {
				add = 15
				target_is_liege_or_above = scope:host
			}
			ai_value_modifier = {
				ai_boldness = 0.2
			}
		}
	}
}

#Blessing of the incense (first phase), sent to every participant
nars_orgio.3005 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = nars_orgio.3005.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { this = scope:host }
				desc = nars_orgio.3005.host.desc
			}
			desc = nars_orgio.3005.guest.desc
		}
	}
	theme = seduction
	override_background = {
		reference = nars_brothel
	}

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = personality_zealous
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:blessing_companion
		animation = admiration
	}

	immediate = {
		scope:activity = {
			activity_host = { save_scope_as = host }
			random_attending_character = {
				limit = { NOT = { this = root } }
				save_scope_as = blessing_companion
			}
		}
		# Refresh rather than stack if an earlier blessing is still running.
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = nars_fertility_blessing }
				remove_character_modifier = nars_fertility_blessing
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = nars_orgio.3005.a
		flavor = nars_orgio.3005.a.flavor
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = nars_fertility_blessing
			years = 2
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = nars_orgio.3005.b
		flavor =  nars_orgio.3005.b.flavor
	}
}

nars_orgio.3003 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = nars_orgio.3003.t
	desc = nars_orgio.3003.desc
	theme = seduction
	override_background = {
		reference = nars_brothel
	}
	override_background = {
		reference = corridor_night
	}
	left_portrait = scope:inviter
	right_portrait = scope:new_guest
	
	trigger = {
		scope:activity = {
			any_attending_character = {
				NOT = { this = root }
				NOT = { is_female = yes }
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		create_character = {
			location = root.capital_province
			template = selian_template.1021
			save_scope_as = new_guest
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			capital_province = { save_scope_as = capital }

			#Find inviter
			scope:activity = {
				random_attending_character = {
					limit = {
						NOR = {
							is_courtier_of = root
							this = root
						}
						is_ai = yes
						is_adult = yes
					}
					alternative_limit = {
					 	is_ai = yes
					 	is_adult = yes
					}
					alternative_limit = { is_adult = yes }
					save_scope_as = inviter
				}
			}
			scope:new_guest = {
				add_character_flag = {
					flag = nars_orgio_new_guest
					days = 30
				}
			}
		}
	}

	#You are very welcome!
	option = {
		name = nars_orgio.3003.a
		
		scope:new_guest = {
			add_to_activity = scope:activity
		}
		reverse_add_opinion = {
			target = scope:new_guest
			modifier = grateful_opinion
			opinion = 15
		}
		reverse_add_opinion = {
			target = scope:inviter
			modifier = grateful_opinion
			opinion = 15
		}
		scope:activity = {
			add_activity_log_entry = {
				key = femalebimbo_thanksforinvite_orgy
				character = scope:new_guest
			}	
		}
	}
	#no, invite only and she not on the list
	option = {
		name = nars_orgio.3003.c
		custom_tooltip = nars_orgio.3003.c.tt
		reverse_add_opinion = {
			target = scope:inviter
			modifier = disappointed_opinion
			opinion = -15
		}
	}
}

			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = impressed_opinion
				opinion = 20
			}
			add_stress = medium_stress_loss
		}
	}
}

	desc = nars_orgio.3010.desc
	theme = seduction
	override_background = {
		reference = nars_brothel
	}
	
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = scheme
		outfit_tags = { no_clothes }
	}	
	center_portrait = {
		character = scope:first_guest
		animation = ecstasy
		outfit_tags = { no_clothes }
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:second_guest
		animation = flirtation_left
		outfit_tags = { no_clothes }
	}
	lower_left_portrait = {
		character = scope:third_guest
		animation = throne_room_applaud_1
		outfit_tags = { no_clothes }
	}
	lower_center_portrait = {
		character = scope:fourth_guest
		animation = throne_room_applaud_1
		outfit_tags = { no_clothes }
	}
	lower_right_portrait = {
		character = scope:fifth_guest
		animation = throne_room_applaud_1
		outfit_tags = { no_clothes }
	}

	immediate = {
		save_scope_as = host
		capital_province = { save_scope_as = background_wilderness_scope } 
		scope:activity = {
			random_attending_character = {
				limit = {
					location = scope:province
					NOT = { this = root }
				}
				save_scope_as = first_guest
			}
		}	
		scope:activity = {
		random_attending_character = {
				limit = {
					location = scope:province
					NOT = { this = scope:first_guest }
					NOT = { this = root }
				}
				save_scope_as = second_guest
			}
		}	
		scope:activity = {
			random_attending_character = {
				limit = {
					location = scope:province
					NOT = { this = scope:first_guest }
					NOT = { this = scope:second_guest }
					NOT = { this = root }
				}
				save_scope_as = third_guest
			}
		}	
		scope:activity = {
		random_attending_character = {
				limit = {
					location = scope:province
					NOT = { this = scope:first_guest }
					NOT = { this = scope:second_guest }
					NOT = { this = scope:third_guest }
					NOT = { this = root }
				}
				save_scope_as = fourth_guest
			}
		}	
		scope:activity = {
			random_attending_character = {
				limit = {
					location = scope:province
					NOT = { this = scope:first_guest }
					NOT = { this = scope:second_guest }
					NOT = { this = scope:third_guest }
					NOT = { this = scope:fourth_guest }
					NOT = { this = root }
				}
				save_scope_as = fifth_guest
			}
		}	
		scope:activity = {
			every_attending_character = {
				add_character_flag = is_naked
			}
		}
	}

	#fuck every hole to impress
	option = {
		trigger = {
			this = scope:host
			scope:host = {
				is_female = no
			}
			scope:activity = {
				any_attending_character = {
					NOT = {	this = scope:host }
					count >= 1
					is_female = yes
				}
			}

#Everyone reacts to the orgy by having random sex, as in might happen accidents (host and guests)
scripted_trigger deviantorgy_3014_can_have_sex_trigger = {
	is_adult = yes
	NOR = {
		has_sexuality = asexual
		has_trait = celibate
	}
}

scripted_trigger deviantorgy_3014_sex_partner_trigger = {
	deviantorgy_3014_can_have_sex_trigger = yes
	NOT = { this = root }
	is_ai = yes
	save_temporary_scope_as = seduction_target
	matching_gender_and_sexuality_trigger = { CHARACTER_1 = scope:seduction_target CHARACTER_2 = root }
}

scripted_trigger deviantorgy_3014_loved_sex_partner_trigger = {
	deviantorgy_3014_sex_partner_trigger = yes
	OR = {
		has_relation_lover = root
		is_consort_of = root
		has_relation_soulmate = root
	}
}

scripted_trigger deviantorgy_3014_regular_sex_partner_trigger = {
	deviantorgy_3014_sex_partner_trigger = yes
	NOR = {
		has_relation_lover = root
		has_relation_soulmate = root
		is_consort_of = root
	}
}

scripted_effect deviantorgy_3017_soulmate_sex_effect = {
	reverse_add_opinion = {
		target = scope:sex_partner
		modifier = love_opinion
		opinion = 20
	}
	add_stress = major_stress_loss
	had_sex_with_effect = { CHARACTER = scope:sex_partner PREGNANCY_CHANCE = pregnancy_chance }
}

		animation = worry
	}

	trigger = {
		exists = scope:activity
		scope:activity = {
			any_attending_character = {
				nars_orgio_3090_secret_holder_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		scope:activity = {
			random_attending_character = {
				limit = { nars_orgio_3090_secret_holder_trigger = yes }
				save_scope_as = secret_holder
				random_secret = {
					limit = { NOT = { is_known_by = root } }
					save_scope_as = secret_to_reveal
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = nars_orgio.3090.a
		scope:secret_to_reveal = {
			reveal_to = root
		}
	}
}

# Intro
nars_religious.10 = {
	type = character_event
	window = big_event_window
	title = nars_religious.10.t
	desc = nars_religious.10.desc
	theme = nars_faith

	center_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = personality_zealous
		camera = camera_body_right
	}

	widget = {
		gui = "event_window_widget_scheme"
		container = "custom_widgets_container"
	}

	trigger = {
	}

	immediate = {
		scope:owner = {
			save_scope_as = owner
		}
		if = {
			limit = { exists = var:s_chosen_deity }
			var:s_chosen_deity = { save_scope_as = s_chosen_deity }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = nars_religious.10.a
        flavor = nars_religious_intro_tt
		trigger = {
			is_landed = no
		}
		show_as_unavailable = { always = yes }
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = nars_focused_devotion_personal_modifier
			years = 5
		}
		scope:scheme = {
			add_scheme_modifier = {
				type = nars_focused_devotion_modifier
				years = 5
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = nars_religious.10.b
        flavor = nars_religious_intro_tt
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = nars_dignified_devotion_personal_modifier
			years = 5
		}
		scope:scheme = {
			add_scheme_modifier = {
				type = nars_dignified_devotion_modifier
				years = 5
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
    
    option = {
		name = nars_religious.10.c
        flavor = nars_religious_intro_tt
		custom_tooltip = no_change_pious_devotion_tt
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}

	after = {
		add_character_flag = study_scheme_intensity_chosen
	}
}

nars_religious.3001 = {
    type = character_event
    title = nars_religious.3001.t
    desc = nars_religious.3001.desc
    theme = nars_faith
    
    left_portrait = {
        character = root
        animation = map_love
        camera = camera_event_left
    }
    right_portrait = {
        character = scope:target
        animation = flirtation_left
        camera = camera_event_right
    }

    trigger = {
        is_ai = no
        NOT = {
            AND = {
                is_male = yes
                has_sexuality = homosexual
            }
        }
        NOT = { has_character_flag = had_nars_religious_3001_event }
    }

    immediate = {
        add_character_flag = had_nars_religious_3001_event

        create_character = {
            location = root.location
            template = priest_character_template
            culture = root.culture
            faith = root.faith
            rite = root.rite
            age = { 18 25 }
            gender_female_chance = 100
            save_scope_as = target
            trait = beauty_good_3
            trait = lustful
            trait = ambitious
            trait = impatient
            trait = seducer
            trait = devoted
            after_creation = {
                remove_bad_traits = yes
                add_character_flag = need_priest_outfit
            }
        }   
    }

    option = {
        name = nars_religious.3001.a # seduce the target
        flavor = nars_religious.3001.a.flavor
        trigger_event = nars_religious.3002
		add_internal_flag = special
        custom_tooltip = {
            text = proceed_seduction_oracle_tt
        }
    }
    
    option = {
        name = nars_religious.3001.b # begone wench
        flavor = nars_religious.3001.b.flavor
        add_stress = minor_stress_gain
    }
}

    option = {
        name = nars_religious.3002.a # take her a lover
        flavor = nars_religious.3002.a.flavor
        owner_seduce_outcome_become_lovers_option_effects = yes
        ai_chance = {
            base = 100
        }
    }

    option = {
        name = nars_religious.3002.b # one night stand
        flavor = nars_religious.3002.b.flavor
        owner_seduce_outcome_just_sex_effects = yes
        ai_chance = {
            base = 0
            ai_value_modifier = {
                ai_greed = 0.25
                ai_vengefulness = 0.25
            }
        }
    }

    after = {
        scope:owner = {
            remove_character_flag = is_naked
        }
        scope:target = {
            remove_character_flag = is_naked
        }
        hidden_effect = {
            random = {
                chance = 10
                trigger_event = {
                    id = nars_religious.3003
                    days = { 7 14 }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

nars_religious.3003 = {
    type = character_event
    hidden = yes

    immediate = {
        var:smitten_oracle = {
            save_scope_as = target
        }

        send_interface_toast = {
            type = event_toast_effect_bad
            title = nars_religious.3003.t
            left_icon = root
            right_icon = scope:target
            custom_tooltip = nars_religious.3003.tt
        }

        scope:target = {
            death = {
                death_reason = death_struck_by_lightning
            }
        }

        add_character_modifier = {
            modifier = nars_temple_fornicator_modifier
            years = 2
        }

        remove_variable = smitten_oracle
    }
}

# Poet / Poet XP gain / Diplomacy Gain
nars_religious.1001 = {
    type = character_event
    title = nars_religious.1001.t
    desc = nars_religious.1001.desc
    theme = nars_faith

    left_portrait = {
        character = root
        animation = thinking
        camera = camera_event_left
    }

    right_portrait = {
        character = scope:teacher
        animation = flirtation
        camera = camera_event_right
    }

    immediate = {
        create_character = {
            location = root.location
            template = priest_character_template
            culture = root.culture
            faith = root.faith
            rite = root.rite
            age = { 18 25 }
            gender_female_chance = 100
            save_scope_as = teacher
            trait = lifestyle_poet
            after_creation = {
                remove_bad_traits = yes
                add_character_flag = need_priest_outfit
            }
        }   
    }
    
    option = {
        name = nars_religious.1001.a # Inhale the poetry
        flavor = nars_religious.1001.a.flavor
        if = {
            limit = {
                has_trait = lifestyle_poet
                has_trait_xp = {
                    trait = lifestyle_poet
                    value >= 100
                }
            }
            add_character_modifier = {
                modifier = nars_poet_inspired_modifier
                months = 3
            }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                has_trait = lifestyle_poet
            }
            add_trait_xp = {
                trait = lifestyle_poet
                value = 15
            }
        }
        else = {
            random = {
                chance = 30
                add_trait = lifestyle_poet
            }
        }
        add_internal_flag = special
    }
    
    option = {
        name = nars_religious.1001.b # study the syntax
        flavor = nars_religious.1001.b.flavor
        trigger = {
            diplomacy < 15
            is_adult = yes
        }
        add_diplomacy_lifestyle_xp = 250
        random = {
            chance = 45
            add_diplomacy_skill = 1
        }
    }

    after  = {
        scope:teacher = {
            silent_disappearance_effect = yes
        }
    }
}

nars_religious.1003 = {
    type = character_event
    title = nars_religious.1003.t
    desc = nars_religious.1003.desc
    theme = nars_faith

    left_portrait = {
        character = root
        animation = throne_room_bow_2
        camera = camera_event_right_away
    }

    option = {
        name = nars_religious.1003.a
        flavor = nars_religious.1003.flavor
        if = {
            limit = {
                has_trait = zealous
            }
            add_piety = massive_piety_gain
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                piety_level >= 3
            }
            add_piety = major_piety_gain
        }
        else = {
            add_piety = medium_piety_gain
        }
    }
}

                }

        }
	}

# Broken Vase
nars_religious.3010 = {
    type = character_event
    title = nars_religious.3010.t
    desc = nars_religious.3010.desc
    theme = nars_faith

    left_portrait = {
        character = root
        animation = scheme
    }

    right_portrait = {
        character = scope:priest
        animation = map_fear
    }

    lower_right_portrait = {
        character = scope:victim
    }

    immediate = {
        create_character = {
            location = root.location
            template = priest_character_template
            culture = root.culture
            faith = root.faith
            rite = root.rite
            age = { 18 25 }
            gender_female_chance = 100
            save_scope_as = priest
            trait = devoted
            after_creation = {
                remove_bad_traits = yes
                add_character_flag = need_priest_outfit
            }
        }
        create_character = {
            location = root.location
            culture = root.culture
            faith = root.faith
            rite = root.rite
            age = { 40 56 }
            gender_female_chance = 0
            save_scope_as = victim
            trait = blind
            trait = intellect_bad_3
            trait = honest
            trait = compassionate
            trait = generous
            trait = devoted
            after_creation = {
                remove_bad_traits = yes
            }
        }   
    }

    # Pay the fine
    option = {
        name = nars_religious.3010.a
        flavor = nars_religious.3010.a.flavor
        remove_short_term_gold = medium_gold_value
        add_piety = minor_piety_gain
    }

    # Frame innocent passerby
    option = {
        name = nars_religious.3010.b
        flavor = nars_religious.3010.b.flavor
        stress_impact = {
			compassionate = minor_stress_impact_gain
			just = minor_stress_impact_gain
            honest = minor_stress_impact_gain
			greedy = minor_stress_impact_loss
            sadistic = minor_stress_impact_loss
            callous = minor_stress_impact_loss
		}
        random_list = {
			50 = {
				modifier = {
					add = 25
					intrigue >= 10
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 25
					intrigue >= 15
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 50
					intrigue >= 20
				}
				custom_tooltip = haao_lie_success_tt
				add_intrigue_lifestyle_xp = 250
			}
			50 = {
				modifier = {
					add = 25
					intrigue < 10
				}
				modifier = {
					add = 25
					intrigue < 5
				}
				custom_tooltip = haao_lie_failure_tt
				add_piety = major_piety_loss
				remove_short_term_gold = medium_gold_value
			}
		}
    }

    after = {
        scope:victim = {
            silent_disappearance_effect = yes
        }
        scope:priest = {
            silent_disappearance_effect = yes
        }
    }
}

        create_character = {
            name = "Stratō"
            gender = male

            location = root.location
            sexuality = bisexual
            random_traits = no

            martial = 10
            stewardship = 10

                add_character_modifier = strato_modifier

                set_father = scope:alnars
                set_mother = scope:mother
                set_house = scope:alnars.house
                
            }
        }

        hidden_effect = {
            scope:consecration_province = {
                set_variable = hide_location_for_saint
            }
            pam_canonize_saint_effect = {	
                CANONIZER = scope:eleios
                CANDIDATE = scope:alnars
                CONSECRATION_PROVINCE = scope:consecration_province
                PRESET = no
            }
            religion:selian_religion = {
                every_faith = {
                    limit = {
                        main_rite = { is_rite_enabled = yes }
                        NOT = { has_saint_character = root }
                    }
                    add_saint = { character = root }
                }
            }
            scope:eleios = {
                save_scope_as = new_landless_adventurer
                create_adventurer_title = {
                    name = d_laamp_nars_0001
                    article = DEFAULT_TITLE_NAME_ARTICLE
                    holder = scope:new_landless_adventurer
                    save_scope_as = new_title
                }
                save_scope_value_as = {
                    name = adventurer_creation_reason
                    value = flag:voluntary
                }
                
                # Starter pack merged with original sibling wealth
                add_gold = { 705 1000 }
                add_prestige = { 705 1000 }
                add_prestige_level = 2
                add_piety_level = 1
                
                while = {
                    count = 2
                    trigger_event = nars_courtiers.9000
                }

                create_title_and_vassal_change = {
                    type = created
                    save_scope_as = change
                    add_claim_on_loss = no
                }
                scope:new_title ?= {
                    create_landless_adventurer_title_history_effect = yes
                    set_variable = {
                        name = adventurer_creation_reason
                        value = scope:adventurer_creation_reason
                    }
                    change_title_holder = {
                        holder = scope:new_landless_adventurer
                        change = scope:change
                    }
                    set_title_name = d_laamp_nars_0001
                    add_title_law = landless_adventurer_succession_law 
                }
                resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:change
                scope:new_title = { save_scope_as = new_landless_adventurer_title }
                
                scope:new_landless_adventurer = {
                    if = {
                        limit = { NOT = { has_government = landless_adventurer_government } }
                        change_government = landless_adventurer_government
                    }
                    if = {
                        limit = { NOT = { has_realm_law = landless_adventurer_succession_law } }
                        add_realm_law_skip_effects = landless_adventurer_succession_law
                    }
                    if = {
                        limit = { has_realm_law = crown_authority_0 }
                        remove_realm_law = crown_authority_0
                    }
                    add_realm_law_skip_effects = camp_purpose_mercenaries
                    nars_naturalis_set_tier_xp_effect = yes
                    
                    domicile = { 
                        nars_laamp_camp_setup_effect = yes
                        change_provisions = starting_provisions_value 
                    }
                }      
                populate_location_with_contracts_effect = {
                    AREA_CHAR = scope:new_landless_adventurer
                    AMOUNT = 5
                }                          
                spawn_army = {
                    name = event_troop_default_name
                    levies = 975
                    men_at_arms = { type = nars_t1 stacks = 12 }
                    men_at_arms = { type = mangonel stacks = 6 }
                    men_at_arms = { type = mangonel stacks = 6 }
                    location = root.location
                    uses_supply = yes
                    inheritable = yes
                }
                hidden_effect = {
                    create_anduril_effect = yes
                    create_narsil_shards_effect = yes
                }
                trigger_event = { 
                    id = nars_intro.0002
                    days = 2
                }
                intro_standard_effect = yes
            }

            scope:ilione = {
                save_scope_as = new_landless_adventurer
                create_adventurer_title = {
                    name = d_laamp_nars_0002
                    article = DEFAULT_TITLE_NAME_ARTICLE
                    holder = scope:new_landless_adventurer
                    save_scope_as = new_title
                }
                save_scope_value_as = {
                    name = adventurer_creation_reason
                    value = flag:voluntary
                }
                
                add_gold = { 705 1000 }
                add_prestige = { 705 1000 }
                add_prestige_level = 2
                add_piety_level = 1

                create_ilyanil_effect = yes

                while = {
                    count = 2
                    trigger_event = nars_courtiers.9000
                }
                
                create_title_and_vassal_change = {
                    type = created
                    save_scope_as = change
                    add_claim_on_loss = no
                }

                    }

                        change = scope:change
                    }
                    set_title_name = d_laamp_nars_0002
                    add_title_law = landless_adventurer_succession_law 
                }
                resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:change
                scope:new_title = { save_scope_as = new_landless_adventurer_title }
                
                scope:new_landless_adventurer = {
                    if = {

                spawn_army = {

nars_intro.0002 = { 
    type = character_event
    title = nars_intro.0002.t
    
    desc = {
        desc = nars_intro.0002.desc        
        first_valid = {
            triggered_desc = {
                trigger = {
                    has_character_modifier = eleios_modifier
                }
                desc = nars_intro.0002.eleios
            }
            triggered_desc = {
                trigger = {
                    has_character_modifier = ilione_modifier
                }
                desc = nars_intro.0002.ilione
            }
            desc = nars_intro.0002.strato
        }
        desc = nars_intro.0002.end
    }

    window = big_event_window
    theme = nars_faith
    
    left_portrait = {
        character = root
        animation = sadness
        camera = camera_body_right
    }
    right_portrait = {
        character = scope:diplomat
        animation = throne_room_messenger_3
        camera = camera_event_right
    }
    lower_center_portrait = {
        character = scope:target_1
    }
    lower_right_portrait = {
        character = scope:target_2
    }
    
    immediate = { 
        play_music_cue = mx_cue_negative       
        father = {
            save_scope_as = alnars
        }
        random_sibling = {
            save_scope_as = target_1
        }
        random_sibling = {
            limit = {
                NOT = { this = scope:target_1 }
            }
            save_scope_as = target_2
        }
        random_courtier = {
            limit = {
                is_adult = yes
                NOT = { this = scope:target_1 }
                NOT = { this = scope:target_2 }
            }
            add_character_flag = need_military_outfit
            save_scope_as = diplomat
        }
        trigger_event = { id = nars_maintenance.0002 days = 1 }
    }
    
    option = {
        clicksound = "event:/DLC/FP2/SFX/UI/fp2_struggle_start_select"
        name = nars_intro.0002.a
        flavor = nars_intro.0002.a.flavor
        add_internal_flag = special
        custom_tooltip = selian_diplomacy.tt
        hidden_effect = {
            selian_diplomacy_entreaty_effect = yes
        }
        ai_chance = { base = 0 }
    }

    option = {
        clicksound = "event:/DLC/FP2/SFX/UI/fp2_struggle_start_select"
        name = nars_intro.0002.b
        flavor = nars_intro.0002.b.flavor
        custom_tooltip = selian_incivility.tt
        ai_chance = { base = 100 }
    }
